-- Toggle this song ID as a favorite

shopframe.ToggleFavorite.Event:connect(function(button)
	local id
	--check if they have unrestricted DJ
	if game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.LocalGamePassCheck:Invoke("UnrestrictedDJ") == false then
		shopframe.DisplayFailure:Fire("You must have Unrestricted DJ Powers to add songs to your Favorites list.")
		return
	end
	
	if button.Parent.Name == "AudioIDSelector" then
		id = button.Parent.InputBox.Text
		if not id:match("%d+") then return end
	elseif button.Parent.Name == "QueueItem" then
		id = tostring(button.Parent.SongID.Value)
	else
		id = button.Parent.Name
	end
	
	if button.Parent.Favorited.Value == false then
		if shopframe.NumFavorites.Value >= 100 then
			shopframe.DisplayFailure:Fire("Sorry, you have reached the maximum number of favorite songs.")
			return
		else
			shopframe.NumFavorites.Value = shopframe.NumFavorites.Value + 1
		end
	else
		shopframe.NumFavorites.Value = shopframe.NumFavorites.Value - 1
	end
	
	if shopframe.Category.Value == "Favorites" then
		shopframe.NavBar.Favorites.Text = "Favorites ("..shopframe.NumFavorites.Value.."/100)"
	end
	
	button.Parent.Favorited.Value = not button.Parent.Favorited.Value
	button.ImageTransparency = button.Parent.Favorited.Value and 0 or 1
	game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.ToggleFavoriteSong:FireServer(id)
	
	if button.Parent.Name == "AudioIDSelector" then
		button.Parent.NoticeText.Text = "Song ID "..id.." ".. (button.Parent.Favorited.Value and "added to" or "removed from") .." favorites".. (button.Parent.Favorited.Value and "!" or ".")
		if shopframe.Category.Value == "Favorites" then
			local i = 0
			repeat
				i = i + 1
				wait(1/15)
			until i >= 10 or shopframe.Category.Value ~= "Favorites" or #data.SongLists.Favorites:GetChildren() == shopframe.NumFavorites.Value
			if shopframe.Category.Value == "Favorites" then
				shopframe.LoadMenu:Fire("Favorites", frame.CanvasPosition)
			end
		end
	end
	
end)

-- When "Add to Queue" button is clicked, submit the request and handle the result
-- (True = song added to queue. False w/ no reason = dont display error. False w/ reason = pop up window with reason. True w/ reason = song added, but it is past the 2 minute threshold and user doesn't have UDJ.)

shopframe:WaitForChild("AudioIDSelector"):WaitForChild("PlayButton").MouseButton1Click:connect(function()
	local id = shopframe.AudioIDSelector.InputBox.Text:match("%d+")
	if not id then
		shopframe.DisplayFailure:Fire("Please enter a valid Audio ID into the box.")
		return
	end
	
	shopframe.AudioIDSelector.NoticeText.Text = "Submitting..."
	local result, reason = game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.RequestDJSong:InvokeServer(id)
	shopframe.AudioIDSelector.NoticeText.Text = ""
	if not result then
		if reason then
			shopframe.DisplayFailure:Fire(reason)
		end
	elseif result == true then
		shopframe.AudioIDSelector.InputBox.Text = " Enter audio ID..."
		shopframe.AudioIDSelector.Favorited.Value = false
		shopframe.AudioIDSelector.FavButton.ImageTransparency = 1
		shopframe.AudioIDSelector.NoticeText.Text = "Song added to queue!"
		if reason then
			--tell user that full length of song will not be played.
			local m = tostring(math.floor(reason / 60))
			local s = tostring(reason % 60)
			if #s == 1 then
				s = "0"..s
			end
			shopframe.SongLengthWindow.Reason.Text = "Song added to queue.\nOnly the first 2 minutes of the song will be played; to play the full-length song ("..m..":"..s.."), purchase Unrestricted DJ Powers!"
		end
	end
	shopframe.LoadMenu:Fire(shopframe.Category.Value, frame.CanvasPosition)
end)

-- Display help window when button is clicked.

local helpbutton = shopframe.AudioIDSelector:WaitForChild("HelpButton")

helpbutton.MouseButton1Click:connect(function()
	shopframe.HelpWindow.Visible = true
end)

helpbutton.MouseEnter:connect(function()
	helpbutton.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,1)
end)

helpbutton.MouseLeave:connect(function()
	helpbutton.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(212, 118, 120)
end)

		if i >= 80 then
			--not processed yet
			shopframe.ProcessingWindow.Header.Text = "Rejoin Server"
			shopframe.ProcessingWindow.Reason.Text = "Your purchase succeeded, but this server has not received the result yet. Try joining a different server if it doesn't work."
		else
			--success
			shopframe.ProcessingWindow.Header.Text = "Success!"
			shopframe.ProcessingWindow.Reason.Text = "Thank you for your purchase of Unrestricted DJ Powers!"
		end
		shopframe.LoadMenu:Fire(shopframe.Category.Value, frame.CanvasPosition)
	end
end)

local plr = game.Players.LocalPlayer
local shopframe = script.Parent:WaitForChild("Shop")
local frame = shopframe:WaitForChild("Items")
local enabled = shopframe:WaitForChild("Enabled")

module.openWindow(script.Parent.Shop)

local char = plr.Character
local torso
if char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
	torso = char.HumanoidRootPart
	shopframe.ShopPos.Value = char.HumanoidRootPart.Position
end

function getItemDataFromID(id)
	for i,v in pairs(game.ReplicatedStorage.Objects:GetChildren()) do
		if v.ItemID.Value == id then
			return v
		end
	end
end

function clearAllButtons()
	for i,v in pairs(frame:GetChildren()) do
		if v.Name ~= "BaseButton" then
			v:Destroy()
		end
	end
	frame.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0,0,0,0)
end


function displayFailureWindow(result,reason) --reason == amount of cash needed
	if result == false then
		
		local failure = shopframe.FailureWindow
		failure.ProductId.Value = 0
		failure.Reason.Text = reason
		failure.NormalButton.Visible = true
		failure.BuyCashButtons.Visible = false
		failure.Visible = true
		repeat wait() until failure.Visible == false
		
	elseif result == "cash" then
		
		local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)
		local cash = data.cash.Value
		local products = game.ReplicatedStorage.CashProducts:GetChildren()
		local bestprod
		local highestprod
		for i,v in pairs(products) do
			if v.Name ~= "30" then
				local diff = tonumber(v.Name) - reason
				if diff >= 0 and (bestprod == nil or tonumber(bestprod.Name) > tonumber(v.Name)) then
					bestprod = v
				end
				if highestprod == nil or tonumber(v.Name) > tonumber(highestprod.Name) then
					highestprod = v
				end
			end
		end
		if bestprod == nil then
			bestprod = highestprod
		end
		
		local failure = shopframe.FailureWindow
		failure.Reason.Text = "You don't have enough Cash to buy this item. Would you like to get more Cash?"
		failure.CashAmount.Value = tonumber(bestprod.Name)
		failure.ProductId.Value = bestprod.Value
		failure.BuyCashButtons.GetCash.Text = "Get +"..bestprod.Name.." Cash!"
		failure.NormalButton.Visible = false
		failure.BuyCashButtons.Visible = true
		failure.Visible = true
		repeat wait() until failure.Visible == false
		
	end
end


function createButton(obj,hassong)
	local button = frame.BaseButton:clone()
	local num = #frame:GetChildren() - 1
	button.Name = obj.Name
	button.ItemID.Value = obj.ItemID.Value
	button.Position = button.Position + UDim2.new(0,0,0,36*num)
	button.TextLabel.Text = string.sub(obj.Name,7)
	if hassong == true then
		button.TextLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.new(.2,1,.2)
	end
	button.Visible = true
	button.Parent = frame
	frame.CanvasSize = frame.CanvasSize + UDim2.new(0,0,0,36)
end

shopframe.LoadMenu.Event:connect(function(category,search)
	local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)
	local objdata = game.ReplicatedStorage.Objects
	if data then
		clearAllButtons()
		if category ~= "Search" then
			shopframe.LoadItemInfo:Fire(nil)
		end
		local count = 0
		for _,obj in pairs(objdata:GetChildren()) do
			if obj.Name:match("music_") and ((search ~= nil and string.sub(string.lower(obj.Name),7):match(string.lower(search))) or (category ~= "Search" and obj.Genre.Value == category)) and obj.ItemCost.Value < 1000000 and not obj:FindFirstChild("NotForSale") then
				local hassong = false
				if data.Objects:FindFirstChild(obj.ItemID.Value) then
					hassong = true
				end
				count = count + 1
				createButton(obj,hassong)
			end
		end
		local warning = shopframe:WaitForChild("ItemsBG"):WaitForChild("WarningLabel")
		if count > 0 then
			warning.Visible = false
		else
			if search ~= nil then
				warning.Text = "There are no CDs which match that search. (Can't find it here? Unrestricted DJ Powers let you play any song!)"
			end
			warning.Visible = true
		end
	end
end)


shopframe.LoadItemInfo.Event:connect(function(button)
	if button ~= nil then
		local id = button.ItemID.Value
		shopframe.SelectedID.Value = id
		shopframe.Desc.ItemName.Text = button.TextLabel.Text
		local item = getItemDataFromID(id)
		shopframe.Desc.ItemCost.Text = "Price: $"..item.ItemCost.Value
		shopframe.Desc.BuyButton.Visible = true
		shopframe.Desc.PreviewButton.Visible = true
	else
		shopframe.SelectedID.Value = 0
		shopframe.Desc.ItemName.Text = "Click on a song to get more info"
		shopframe.Desc.ItemCost.Text = ""
		shopframe.Desc.BuyButton.Visible = false
		shopframe.Desc.PreviewButton.Visible = false
	end
end)


shopframe.PreviewMusic.Event:connect(function()
	local item = getItemDataFromID(shopframe.SelectedID.Value)
	if item then
		if script.Parent:FindFirstChild("MusicPreview") then
			script.Parent.MusicPreview:Stop()
			script.Parent.MusicPreview:Destroy()
		end
		if item.AssetID.Value == 142329328 then --WDTFS
			return
		end
		local sound = Instance.new("Sound")
		sound.Name = "MusicPreview"
		sound.Volume = .56 - item.Modifier.Value
		sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://"..item.AssetID.Value
		sound.Parent = script.Parent
		sound:Play()
		game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(sound,45)
		wait(40)
		if sound then
			local vol = sound.Volume
			for i=1,20 do
				if sound then
					sound.Volume = sound.Volume - vol/20
					wait(.1)
				end
			end
			if sound then
				sound:Stop()
				sound:Destroy()
			end
		end
	end
end)


shopframe.BuyRequest.Event:connect(function(button)
	if enabled.Value == true then
		enabled.Value = false
		local oldbg = button.BackgroundColor3
		local oldborder = button.BorderColor3
		button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(.6,.6,.6)
		button.BorderColor3 = Color3.new(.1,.1,.1)
		local result,reason = game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.PurchasePermanentItem:InvokeServer( shopframe.SelectedID.Value)
		if result == true then
			local sound = Instance.new("Sound",plr.PlayerGui)
			sound.Name = "ShopBuySuccess"
			sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://"..game.ReplicatedStorage.AssetIDs[sound.Name].Value
			sound:Play()
			game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(sound,3)
		else
			displayFailureWindow(result,reason)
		end
		for i,v in pairs(frame:GetChildren()) do
			if v.ItemID.Value == shopframe.SelectedID.Value then
				v.TextLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.new(.2,1,.2)
			end
		end
		button.BackgroundColor3 = oldbg
		button.BorderColor3 = oldborder
		enabled.Value = true
	end
end)

	asset = marketplace:GetProductInfo(assetid)

	repeat wait(60) pcall(function()

	end)
	until asset ~= nil

			gui.Desc.ItemCostDiscounted.Text = asset.PriceInRobux.." R$"

			gui.Desc.ItemCost.Text = "Price: "..asset.PriceInRobux.." R$"
			gui.Desc.DiscountSlash.Visible = false
			gui.Desc.ItemCostDiscounted.Text = ""
			gui.Desc.Savings.Text = ""
			
			gui.Desc.ItemCost.TextStrokeTransparency = .7
			gui.Desc.ItemCost.TextTransparency = 0
		end
	else
		gui.Desc.ItemCost.Text = "(failed to load price)"
	end
end

function onMouseLeave()
	hoverimg.Visible = false
end

	local selection = gui:WaitForChild("GamepadSelection")
	selection.Changed:connect(function(obj)
		if obj == script.Parent then
			onMouseEnter()

			onMouseLeave()

local regularimg = script.Parent.Image
local hoverimg = script.Parent:WaitForChild("HoverImage")

local desc = "Principals with this pass can give out detentions, suspend players, make overhead announcements, alter the school schedule, and prevent other players from being on the Principal team! Click on the 'Enforcer's Powers' button in the Options menu to use this."
local itemname = "Enforcer's Powers"

local marketplace = game:GetService("MarketplaceService")
local assetid = game.ReplicatedStorage.GamePasses[script.Parent.Name].Value

local gui = script.Parent.Parent.Parent --the Frame frame
local plr = game.Players.LocalPlayer

script.Parent.MouseButton1Click:connect(function()
	marketplace:PromptPurchase(plr,assetid)
end)

local asset 

pcall(function()
	asset = marketplace:GetProductInfo(assetid)
end)

if asset == nil then
	repeat wait(60) pcall(function()
		asset = marketplace:GetProductInfo(assetid)
	end)
	until asset ~= nil
end

local baseprice = game.ReplicatedStorage.GamePasses[script.Parent.Name].BasePrice.Value

function onMouseEnter()
	hoverimg.Visible = true
	gui.Desc.ItemDesc.Text = desc
	gui.Desc.ItemName.Text = itemname
	if asset then
		if asset.PriceInRobux < baseprice then --sale
			gui.Desc.ItemCost.Text = "Price: "..baseprice.." R$"
			gui.Desc.DiscountSlash.Visible = true
			gui.Desc.ItemCostDiscounted.Text = asset.PriceInRobux.." R$"
			gui.Desc.Savings.Text = "You save: "..baseprice - asset.PriceInRobux.." R$!"
			
			gui.Desc.ItemCost.TextStrokeTransparency = 1
			gui.Desc.ItemCost.TextTransparency = .5
		else
			gui.Desc.ItemCost.Text = "Price: "..asset.PriceInRobux.." R$"
			gui.Desc.DiscountSlash.Visible = false
			gui.Desc.ItemCostDiscounted.Text = ""
			gui.Desc.Savings.Text = ""
			
			gui.Desc.ItemCost.TextStrokeTransparency = .7
			gui.Desc.ItemCost.TextTransparency = 0
		end
	else
		gui.Desc.ItemCost.Text = "(failed to load price)"
	end
end

function onMouseLeave()
	hoverimg.Visible = false
end

if plr:FindFirstChild("GamepadMode") then
	local selection = gui:WaitForChild("GamepadSelection")
	selection.Changed:connect(function(obj)
		if obj == script.Parent then
			onMouseEnter()
		else
			onMouseLeave()
		end
	end)
end

script.Parent.MouseEnter:connect(onMouseEnter)

script.Parent.MouseLeave:connect(onMouseLeave)y

local plr = game.Players.LocalPlayer

local module = require(game.ReplicatedStorage.Modules.InterfaceModule)
module.openWindow(gui)

local assets = { --store the success descriptions
	["ClubRed"] = "You have successfully purchased Club Red DJ Access! You can now play ANY song ID on ROBLOX at the Club Red DJ stand. (On the ROBLOX website, go to Develop --> Library --> Audio to find audio that you like.) Enjoy the party!",
	["UnrestrictedDJ"] = "You have successfully purchased Unrestricted DJ Powers! You can now play ANY song ID on ROBLOX at Club Red, as well as take advantage of all the special bonus abilities that Unrestricted DJs receive! (On the ROBLOX website, go to Develop --> Library --> Audio to find audio that you like.) Enjoy!",
	["VIP"] = "You have successfully purchased Gold VIP! You will now earn extra RHS Cash per minute and have gold-colored chat. You also have access to VIP-only name colors; hover over the chat box and click 'Extras' to see them! (You may need to reset your character first)",
	["EnforcersPowers"] = "You have successfully purchased Enforcer's Powers! Become the Enforcer by clicking 'Enforcer's Powers' under the Options menu. (There can only be one Enforcer per server.) To punish unruly players, click directly on their character and select an option. Make sure you use your powers responsibly - if you punish students for no reason, your powers will be taken away!"
}

gui:WaitForChild("LoadSection").Event:connect(function(section)
	for _,v in pairs(gui:GetChildren()) do
		if v.Name:match("SEC_") then
			if v.Name == "SEC_"..section then
				v.Visible = true
			else
				v.Visible = false
			end
		end
	end
	
	for _,v in pairs(gui.Navigation:GetChildren()) do
		if v.Name == section then
			v.BackgroundTransparency = .1
			v.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,1)
		else
			v.BackgroundTransparency = .4
			v.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,0)
		end
	end
	
	gui.Desc.ItemName.Text = "Welcome!"
	gui.Desc.ItemDesc.Text = [[Here you can buy RHS Cash and special Game Passes to enhance your gameplay experience.
		
Hover over any icon to see its description and price, and click on the icon to  open the purchase prompt.]]
	gui.Desc.ItemCost.Text = ""
	gui.Desc.ItemCostDiscounted.Text = ""
	gui.Desc.DiscountSlash.Visible = false
	gui.Desc.Savings.Text = ""
end)

marketplace.PromptPurchaseFinished:connect(function(plrpurchased,purchaseId,isPurchased)
	if plrpurchased == plr and isPurchased then
		local successdesc
		
		for name,desc in pairs(assets) do
			if game.ReplicatedStorage.GamePasses[name].Value == purchaseId then
				successdesc = desc
			end
		end
		
		if successdesc == nil then
			for i,v in pairs(game.ReplicatedStorage.CashProducts:GetChildren()) do
				if v.Value == purchaseId then
					successdesc = "You have successfully purchased +"..v.Name.." RHS Cash. You can use RHS Cash to purchase a variety of special items throughout the game. Enjoy!"
				end
			end
		end
		
		if successdesc == nil then
			successdesc = ""
		end
		
		gui.PurchaseSuccess.Desc.Text = successdesc
		gui.PurchaseSuccess.Visible = true
	end
end)

if plr:FindFirstChild("GamepadMode") then
	coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(function()
		local gamepadselection = gui:WaitForChild("GamepadSelection")
		local guiservice = game:GetService("GuiService")
		while true do
			wait(1/30)
			if gamepadselection.Value ~= guiservice.SelectedObject then
				gamepadselection.Value = guiservice.SelectedObject
			end
		end
	end))
end

			gui:WaitForChild("SaleLabel").Visible = false
		end
		wait(60)
	end

=== chunk=419 type=PROP offset=279847 runSha256=d86670b1d842eea436192ee2bea3b90d8e3e7be2d1256e9facd02eb0052a83d3 ===
--remember, pages are 1, 4, 7, and 10 (+2)

--player's data --> Outfits --> outfits named 1-12

--last few things: check the SafeToExit things, then we're good to go??

local plr = game.Players.LocalPlayer
local frame = script.Parent:WaitForChild("Frame")
local outfitslots = frame:WaitForChild("OutfitSlots")
local outfitpages = frame:WaitForChild("OutfitPages")
local inputs = frame:WaitForChild("OutfitInputs")
local enabled = frame:WaitForChild("Enabled")

local marketplace = game:GetService("MarketplaceService")

local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:WaitForChild(plr.userId)

local canpurchase = true --stop multi-purchases

local module = require(game.ReplicatedStorage.Modules.InterfaceModule)
module.openWindow(script.Parent.Frame)

function getItemDataFromID(id)
	for i,v in pairs(game.ReplicatedStorage.Objects:GetChildren()) do
		if v.ItemID.Value == id then
			return v
		end
	end
end


function displayWindow(windowname,reason) --reason is optional and only for the errorwarning
	enabled.Value = false
	local window = frame:FindFirstChild(windowname)
	if window then
		if reason ~= nil then
			window.Reason.Text = reason
		end
		window.Visible = true
		repeat wait() until window.Visible == false
	end
	enabled.Value = true
end

function loadSlots(num) --1/4/7/10
	local slot = tonumber(num)
	frame.SelectedPage.Value = slot
	
	for i,v in pairs(outfitslots:GetChildren()) do
		if v.Name ~= "BaseSlot" then
			v:Destroy()
		end
	end
	
	for i=slot,slot+2 do
		local outfit = data.Outfits:FindFirstChild(i)
		if outfit then
			local gui = outfitslots.BaseSlot:clone()
			gui.OutfitName.Text = outfit.OutfitName.Value
			if outfit.OutfitName.Value == "" then
				gui.OutfitName.Text = "Empty"
				gui.Load.Visible = false
			end
			gui.NumberLabel.Text = "#"..i
			gui.Position = gui.Position + UDim2.new((#outfitslots:GetChildren() - 1)*.33,0,0,0)
			gui.Name = i
			gui.Parent = outfitslots
			gui.Visible = true
		end
	end
	setOutfitPageColors()
end

function setOutfitPageColors()
	for _,button in pairs(outfitpages:GetChildren()) do
		button.BackgroundTransparency = .5
		if button.Name ~= "OutfitsPageBaseSlot" then --has outfit page
			button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(140/255, 221/255, 1)
			--button.ItemCost.Text = ""
		end
		if frame.SelectedPage.Value == tonumber(button.Name) then
			button.BackgroundTransparency = .2
		end
	end
end

data.Objects.ChildAdded:connect(function(child)
	if tonumber(child.Name) == outfitpages["4"].ItemID.Value or tonumber(child.Name) == outfitpages["7"].ItemID.Value or tonumber(child.Name) == outfitpages["10"].ItemID.Value then
		setOutfitPageColors()
	end
end)

frame.LoadOutfitPage.Event:connect(function(num)
	loadSlots(num)
end)

frame.ChangeSelectedSlot.Event:connect(function(num)
	frame.SelectedSlot.Value = num
end)

function checkIfSafeToExit() --for loading or closing - (if input boxes are NOT blank, or input boxes do NOT match an already saved outfit)
	return true
end

--"confirmed" - nil means display a window - true means it's okay to do the action

frame.LoadOutfit.Event:connect(function(confirmed) --load the outfit IDs stored in the SelectedSlot
	local check = checkIfSafeToExit()
	if not confirmed and enabled.Value == true and check == false then
		enabled.Value = false
		displayWindow("LoadWarning")
		enabled.Value = true
	elseif confirmed == true or check == true then
		local slot = frame.SelectedSlot.Value
		local outfit = data.Outfits:FindFirstChild(slot)
		if outfit then
			
			for _,v in pairs(inputs:GetChildren()) do
				if v.Name == "RemoveShirt" then
					v.Enabled.Value = outfit.RemoveShirt.Value
				else
					v.InputBox.Text = outfit[v.Name].Value
					if outfit[v.Name].Value == 0 then
						v.InputBox.Text = ""
					end
				end
			end
			
			frame.WearOutfit:Fire()
			
		end
	end
end)

frame.CloseWindow.Event:connect(function(confirmed)
	local check = checkIfSafeToExit()
	if not confirmed and enabled.Value == true and check == false then
		displayWindow("CloseWarning")
	elseif confirmed == true or check == true then
		module.closeWindow(script.Parent)
	end
end)

frame.WearOutfit.Event:connect(function()
	if enabled.Value == true then
		enabled.Value = false
		local tabletosend = {} --["NameOfInput"] = ID
		for _,v in pairs(inputs:GetChildren()) do
			if v.Name == "RemoveShirt" then
				tabletosend[v.Name] = v.Enabled.Value
			else
				tabletosend[v.Name] = v.InputBox.Text
			end
		end
		local request,statuses = game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.WearOutfit:InvokeServer(tabletosend)
		if request == true then
			for _,v in pairs(inputs:GetChildren()) do
				v.InputBox.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,1)
			end
			displayWindow("SuccessWindow","Outfit worn successfully!")
		elseif request == false then
			if statuses ~= nil then
				for _,v in pairs(inputs:GetChildren()) do
					if tostring(statuses[v.Name]):match("invalid") or tostring(statuses[v.Name]):match("admin") then
						v.InputBox.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,.4,.4)
					end
				end
			end
			displayWindow("ErrorWarning","You have entered at least 1 invalid asset ID. Please correct the invalid IDs.")
		else
			displayWindow("ErrorWarning","Please wait a few more moments before trying this again.")
		end
		enabled.Value = true
	end
end)

frame.LoadOutfitPage:Fire(1)
setOutfitPageColors()

	enabled.Value = true
end

function loadSlots(num) --1/4/7/10
	local slot = tonumber(num)
	frame.SelectedPage.Value = slot
	
	for i,v in pairs(outfitslots:GetChildren()) do
		if v.Name ~= "BaseSlot" then
			v:Destroy()
		end
	end
	
	for i=slot,slot+2 do
		local outfit = data.Outfits:FindFirstChild(i)
		if outfit then
			local gui = outfitslots.BaseSlot:clone()
			gui.OutfitName.Text = outfit.OutfitName.Value
			gui.NumberLabel.Text = "#"..i
			gui.Position = gui.Position + UDim2.new((#outfitslots:GetChildren() - 1)*.33,0,0,0)
			gui.Name = i
			gui.Parent = outfitslots
			gui.Visible = true
		end
	end
	setOutfitPageColors()
end

function setOutfitPageColors()
	for _,button in pairs(outfitpages:GetChildren()) do
		button.BackgroundTransparency = .5
		if button.Name ~= "OutfitsPageBaseSlot" then --has outfit page
			button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(140/255, 221/255, 1)
			--button.ItemCost.Text = ""
		end
		if frame.SelectedPage.Value == tonumber(button.Name) then
			button.BackgroundTransparency = .2
		end
	end
end

data.Objects.ChildAdded:connect(function(child)
	if tonumber(child.Name) == outfitpages["4"].ItemID.Value or tonumber(child.Name) == outfitpages["7"].ItemID.Value or tonumber(child.Name) == outfitpages["10"].ItemID.Value then
		setOutfitPageColors()
	end
end)

frame.LoadOutfitPage.Event:connect(function(num)
	loadSlots(num)
end)

frame.ChangeSelectedSlot.Event:connect(function(num)
	frame.SelectedSlot.Value = num
end)

function checkIfSafeToExit() --for loading or closing - (if input boxes are NOT blank, or input boxes do NOT match an already saved outfit)
	local inputsblank = true
	for _,v in pairs(inputs:GetChildren()) do
		if v.Name ~= "RemoveShirt" and v.InputBox.Text ~= "" then
			inputsblank = false
		end
	end
	
	if inputsblank == false then
		for _,outfit in pairs(data.Outfits:GetChildren()) do
			local matching = true
			for _,v in pairs(inputs:GetChildren()) do
				local val = outfit[v.Name].Value
				if val == 0 then
					val = ""
				end
				if v.Name ~= "RemoveShirt" and v.InputBox.Text ~= tostring(val) then
					matching = false
				end
			end
			if matching == true then return true end
		end
		return false
	else
		return true
	end
end

function checkIfSafeToSave() --(if slot is NOT empty, or outfit in that slot is NOT the same)
	local slot = frame.SelectedSlot.Value
	local outfit = data.Outfits:FindFirstChild(slot)
	if outfit then
		if outfit.OutfitName.Value == "" then return true end
		for _,v in pairs(inputs:GetChildren()) do
			if v.Name == "RemoveShirt" then
				if v.Enabled.Value ~= outfit.RemoveShirt.Value then return false end
			else
				local val = tostring(outfit[v.Name].Value)
				if val == 0 then
					val = ""
				end
				if v.InputBox.Text ~= tostring(val) then return false end
			end
		end
		return true
	end
end

--"confirmed" - nil means display a window - true means it's okay to do the action

frame.LoadOutfit.Event:connect(function(confirmed) --load the outfit IDs stored in the SelectedSlot
	local check = checkIfSafeToExit()
	if not confirmed and enabled.Value == true and check == false then
		enabled.Value = false
		displayWindow("LoadWarning")
		enabled.Value = true
	elseif confirmed == true or check == true then
		local slot = frame.SelectedSlot.Value
		local outfit = data.Outfits:FindFirstChild(slot)
		if outfit then
			
			for _,v in pairs(inputs:GetChildren()) do
				if v.Name == "RemoveShirt" then
					v.Enabled.Value = outfit.RemoveShirt.Value
				else
					v.InputBox.Text = outfit[v.Name].Value
					if outfit[v.Name].Value == 0 then
						v.InputBox.Text = ""
					end
				end
			end
			
		end
	end
end)

frame.CloseWindow.Event:connect(function(confirmed)
	local check = checkIfSafeToExit()
	if not confirmed and enabled.Value == true and check == false then
		displayWindow("CloseWarning")
	elseif confirmed == true or check == true then
		module.closeWindow(script.Parent)
	end
end)

frame.SaveOutfit.Event:connect(function(confirmed)
	local check = checkIfSafeToSave()
	if not confirmed and enabled.Value == true and check == false then
		displayWindow("SaveWarning")
	elseif confirmed == true or check == true then
		local slot = frame.SelectedSlot.Value
		local outfit = data.Outfits:FindFirstChild(slot)
		if outfit then
			
			--[[for _,v in pairs(inputs:GetChildren()) do --hah, we gotta FE this
				if v.Name == "RemoveShirt" then
					 outfit.RemoveShirt.Value = v.Enabled.Value
				else
					 outfit[v.Name].Value = v.InputBox.Text
				end
			end]]
			
			local tabletosend = {} --["NameOfInput"] = ID
			for _,v in pairs(inputs:GetChildren()) do
				if v.Name == "RemoveShirt" then
					tabletosend[v.Name] = v.Enabled.Value
				else
					tabletosend[v.Name] = v.InputBox.Text
				end
			end
			local request,statuses = game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.SaveOutfit:InvokeServer(tabletosend,slot)
			if request == true then
				for _,v in pairs(inputs:GetChildren()) do
					v.InputBox.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,1)
				end
				frame.LoadOutfitPage:Fire(frame.SelectedPage.Value)
				displayWindow("SuccessWindow","Outfit saved successfully!")
			elseif request == false then
				if statuses ~= nil then
					for _,v in pairs(inputs:GetChildren()) do
						if tostring(statuses[v.Name]):match("invalid") or tostring(statuses[v.Name]):match("admin") then
							v.InputBox.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,.4,.4)
						end
					end
				end
				displayWindow("ErrorWarning","You have entered at least 1 invalid asset ID. Please correct the invalid IDs.")
			else
				displayWindow("ErrorWarning","To avoid straining the server, please wait a few moments to try this again.")
			end
			
		end
	end
end)

frame.WearOutfit.Event:connect(function()
	if enabled.Value == true then
		enabled.Value = false
		local tabletosend = {} --["NameOfInput"] = ID
		for _,v in pairs(inputs:GetChildren()) do
			if v.Name == "RemoveShirt" then
				tabletosend[v.Name] = v.Enabled.Value
			else
				tabletosend[v.Name] = v.InputBox.Text
			end
		end
		local request,statuses = game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.WearOutfit:InvokeServer(tabletosend)
		if request == true then
			for _,v in pairs(inputs:GetChildren()) do
				v.InputBox.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,1)
			end
			displayWindow("SuccessWindow","Outfit worn successfully!")
		elseif request == false then
			if statuses ~= nil then
				for _,v in pairs(inputs:GetChildren()) do
					if tostring(statuses[v.Name]):match("invalid") or tostring(statuses[v.Name]):match("admin") then
						v.InputBox.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,.4,.4)
					end
				end
			end
			displayWindow("ErrorWarning","You have entered at least 1 invalid asset ID. Please correct the invalid IDs.")
		else
			displayWindow("ErrorWarning","Please wait a few more moments before trying this again.")
		end
		enabled.Value = true
	end
end)

	if not v:FindFirstChild("NotForSale") and not (v:FindFirstChild("ReleaseTime") and v.ReleaseTime.Value > game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value and game.PlaceId == 9689581) and not (v:FindFirstChild("TimeToGoOffSale") and v.TimeToGoOffSale.Value < game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value) then
		local button = gui.Items.BaseButton:clone() --make new button for each item
		button.Parent = gui.Items
		button.Name = v.Name
		button.ItemID.Value = v.ItemID.Value
		button.Image = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id="..v.DecalID.Value
		button.Position = UDim2.new(0, xv*75, 0, yv*75)
		if v:FindFirstChild("TwitterCode") then
			local bird = script.TwitterBird:clone()
			bird.Parent = button
			bird.Visible = true
		end
		local oldcost = v:FindFirstChild(oldcostname)
		if oldcost and currenttime < sale_end_time and not data.Objects:FindFirstChild(v.ItemID.Value) then
			local percent = math.ceil(-(v.ItemCost.Value - oldcost.Value) / oldcost.Value * 100)
			if percent == 34 then
				percent = 33
			end
			local oc = Instance.new("IntValue")
			oc.Name = "LocalOldCost"
			oc.Value = oldcost.Value
			oc.Parent = button
			button.DiscountPercent.Text = percent.."%"
			button.DiscountBanner.Visible = true
		end
		if v:FindFirstChild("ReleaseTime") and game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value - v.ReleaseTime.Value < 86400*21 and not v:FindFirstChild("TimeToGoOffSale") then
			local newb = script.NewBanner:clone()
			newb.Parent = button
			newb.Visible = true
		elseif v:FindFirstChild("TimeToGoOffSale") then
			local b = script.LimitedBanner:clone()
			b.Parent = button
			b.Visible = true
			local offsaletime = Instance.new("IntValue")
			offsaletime.Name = "OffSaleTime"
			offsaletime.Value = v.TimeToGoOffSale.Value
			offsaletime.Parent = button
		end
		xv = xv + 1
		if xv == 4 then
			xv = 0
			yv = yv + 1
		end
		button.Visible = true
	end
end

function setButtonColors()
	for _,button in pairs(gui.Items:GetChildren()) do
		if button.Name ~= "BaseButton" then
			if data.Objects:FindFirstChild(button.ItemID.Value) then --has object
				button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(.5,1,.5)
			else
				button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,1)
			end
			if gui.SelectedItem.Value == button.ItemID.Value then
				button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,.25)
			end
		end
	end
end


function displayFailureWindow(result,reason) --reason == amount of cash needed
	if result == false then
		
		local failure = gui.FailureWindow
		failure.ProductId.Value = 0
		failure.Reason.Text = reason
		failure.NormalButton.Visible = true
		failure.BuyCashButtons.Visible = false
		failure.Visible = true
		repeat wait() until failure.Visible == false
		
	elseif result == "cash" then
		
		local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)
		local cash = data.cash.Value
		local products = game.ReplicatedStorage.CashProducts:GetChildren()
		local bestprod
		local highestprod
		for i,v in pairs(products) do
			if v.Name ~= "30" then
				local diff = tonumber(v.Name) - reason
				if diff >= 0 and (bestprod == nil or tonumber(bestprod.Name) > tonumber(v.Name)) then
					bestprod = v
				end
				if highestprod == nil or tonumber(v.Name) > tonumber(highestprod.Name) then
					highestprod = v
				end
			end
		end
		if bestprod == nil then
			bestprod = highestprod
		end
		
		local failure = gui.FailureWindow
		failure.Reason.Text = "You don't have enough Cash to buy this item. Would you like to get more Cash?"
		failure.CashAmount.Value = tonumber(bestprod.Name)
		failure.ProductId.Value = bestprod.Value
		failure.BuyCashButtons.GetCash.Text = "Get +"..bestprod.Name.." Cash!"
		failure.NormalButton.Visible = false
		failure.BuyCashButtons.Visible = true
		failure.Visible = true
		repeat wait() until failure.Visible == false
		
	end
end


gui.BuyRequest.Event:connect(function(button)
	if enabled.Value == true then
		enabled.Value = false
		local oldbg = button.BackgroundColor3
		local oldborder = button.BorderColor3
		button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(.6,.6,.6)
		button.BorderColor3 = Color3.new(.1,.1,.1)
		local result,reason = game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.PurchasePermanentItem:InvokeServer( gui.SelectedItem.Value) --the item ID
		if result == true then
			local sound = Instance.new("Sound",plr.PlayerGui)
			sound.Name = "ShopBuySuccess"
			sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://"..game.ReplicatedStorage.AssetIDs[sound.Name].Value
			sound:Play()
			game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(sound,3)
		else
			displayFailureWindow(result,reason)
		end
		button.BackgroundColor3 = oldbg
		button.BorderColor3 = oldborder
		enabled.Value = true
	end
end)

gui.LoadMenu.Event:connect(function(button) --selected button, get item data
	local itemdata = game.ReplicatedStorage.Objects:FindFirstChild(button.Name)
	local itemname = string.sub(button.Name,#category.Value + 2)
	gui.Desc.ItemName.Text = itemname
	gui.Desc.ItemDesc.Text = itemdata.ItemDesc.Value
	gui.Desc.ItemCostDiscounted.Text = ""
	gui.Desc.DiscountSlash.Visible = false
	gui.Desc.Savings.Text = ""
	gui.Desc.LimitedTimeNotice.Text = ""
	
	gui.Desc.ItemCost.TextStrokeTransparency = .7
	gui.Desc.ItemCost.TextTransparency = 0
	
	if not itemdata:FindFirstChild("TwitterCode") then
		if data.Objects:FindFirstChild(itemdata.ItemID.Value) then
			gui.Desc.ItemCost.Text = "You own this item"
			gui.Desc.BuyButton.Visible = false
			gui.Desc.TwitterNotice.Text = ""
		else
			gui.Desc.ItemCost.Text = "Price: $"..itemdata.ItemCost.Value
			gui.Desc.BuyButton.Visible = true
			gui.Desc.TwitterNotice.Text = ""
			if button:FindFirstChild("LocalOldCost") then
				gui.Desc.ItemCost.Text = "Price: $"..button.LocalOldCost.Value
				gui.Desc.DiscountSlash.Visible = true
				gui.Desc.ItemCostDiscounted.Text = "$"..itemdata.ItemCost.Value
				gui.Desc.Savings.Text = "You save: $"..button.LocalOldCost.Value - itemdata.ItemCost.Value.."!"
				
				gui.Desc.ItemCost.TextStrokeTransparency = 1
				gui.Desc.ItemCost.TextTransparency = .5
			end
			if button:FindFirstChild("OffSaleTime") then
				local timeleft = button.OffSaleTime.Value - game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value
				local days = math.floor(timeleft / 86400)
				local timeleftstr = (days >= 2 and (days.." days")) or (days == 1 and "1 day") or "less than 1 day"
				gui.Desc.LimitedTimeNotice.Text = "Off-sale in: "..timeleftstr
			end
		end
	else
		if data.Objects:FindFirstChild(itemdata.ItemID.Value) then
			gui.Desc.TwitterNotice.Text = "You've unlocked this item from being an RHS Fan Club member!"
			gui.Desc.ItemCost.Text = ""
			gui.Desc.BuyButton.Visible = false
		else
			gui.Desc.TwitterNotice.Text = "Join the RHS Fan Club to unlock this item! (Link in the game description)"
			gui.Desc.ItemCost.Text = ""
			gui.Desc.BuyButton.Visible = false
		end
	end
	
	for i,v in pairs(gui.Items:GetChildren()) do
		v.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,1)
	end
	button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,.25)
	gui.SelectedItem.Value = button.ItemID.Value
	setButtonColors()
end)

function displayFailureWindow(result,reason) --reason == amount of cash needed
	if result == false then
		
		local failure = gui.FailureWindow
		failure.ProductId.Value = 0
		failure.Reason.Text = reason
		failure.NormalButton.Visible = true
		failure.BuyCashButtons.Visible = false
		failure.Visible = true
		repeat wait() until failure.Visible == false
		
	elseif result == "cash" then
		
		local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)
		local cash = data.cash.Value
		local products = game.ReplicatedStorage.CashProducts:GetChildren()
		local bestprod
		local highestprod
		for i,v in pairs(products) do
			if v.Name ~= "30" then
				local diff = tonumber(v.Name) - reason
				if diff >= 0 and (bestprod == nil or tonumber(bestprod.Name) > tonumber(v.Name)) then
					bestprod = v
				end
				if highestprod == nil or tonumber(v.Name) > tonumber(highestprod.Name) then
					highestprod = v
				end
			end
		end
		if bestprod == nil then
			bestprod = highestprod
		end
		
		local failure = gui.FailureWindow
		failure.Reason.Text = "You don't have enough Cash to buy this item. Would you like to get more Cash?"
		failure.CashAmount.Value = tonumber(bestprod.Name)
		failure.ProductId.Value = bestprod.Value
		failure.BuyCashButtons.GetCash.Text = "Get +"..bestprod.Name.." Cash!"
		failure.NormalButton.Visible = false
		failure.BuyCashButtons.Visible = true
		failure.Visible = true
		repeat wait() until failure.Visible == false
		
	end
end

		end

	end
end)

function getAvailableItemCount(id)
	local count = 0
	for _,v in pairs(data.Objects:GetChildren()) do
		if tonumber(v.Name) == id then
			count = count + 1
		end
	end
	local furnbought = data.FurnitureCost:FindFirstChild(id)
	if furnbought then
		count = count + furnbought.PendingSales.Value + furnbought.Value --items in Objects - sold items - added items (or + removed items)
	end
	return count
end

function loadCategory(categoryname)

		local yv = 0

		savedcategory.Value = categoryname

			end
		end

				button.Position = UDim2.new(0, xv*75, 0, yv*75)
				if v:FindFirstChild("TwitterCode") then
					local bird = script.TwitterBird:clone()
					bird.Parent = button
					bird.Visible = true
				end
				if v:FindFirstChild("ReleaseTime") and game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value - v.ReleaseTime.Value < 86400*21 then
					local newb = script.NewBanner:clone()
					newb.Parent = button
					newb.Visible = true
				end
				xv = xv + 1
				if xv == 4 then
					xv = 0
					yv = yv + 1
				end
				button.Visible = true
			end
		end
		
		gui.Items.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0,0,0,(yv + 1) * 75)
		gui.LoadItemInfo:Fire()
		setButtonColors()

			if v.Name == categoryname then
				v.BackgroundTransparency = 0
			else
				v.BackgroundTransparency = .5
			end
		end
	end
end

local plr = game.Players.LocalPlayer
local mp = game:GetService("MarketplaceService")

local gui = script.Parent:WaitForChild("Frame")

local pantsid = gui:WaitForChild("PantsID")

wait(.09)

--print(game:GetService("MarketplaceService"):GetProductInfo(581285249).Creator.CreatorTargetId)

function getClothingId(valueobj)
	if valueobj.Value ~= 0 then
		local asset
		local origid = valueobj.Value
		pcall(function()
			asset = mp:GetProductInfo(origid)
		end)
		if asset then
			local creator = asset.Creator.CreatorTargetId
			local count = 0
			local success
			local clothingasset
			local groups
			repeat
				pcall(function() clothingasset = mp:GetProductInfo(origid+(count+1)) end)
				if clothingasset and (clothingasset.AssetTypeId == 11 or clothingasset.AssetTypeId == 12) then
					if clothingasset.Creator.CreatorTargetId == creator then
						--surefire hit
						success = true
					elseif clothingasset.Creator.CreatorType == "Group" then
						if groups == nil then
							pcall(function()
								groups = game:GetService("GroupService"):GetGroupsAsync(creator)
							end)
						end
						if groups then
							for _,entry in pairs(groups) do
								if entry["Id"] == clothingasset.Creator.CreatorTargetId then
									success = true
									break
								end
							end
						end
					else
						clothingasset = nil
					end
				else
					clothingasset = nil
				end
				count = count + 1
			until success == true or count > 25
			if success == true then
				valueobj.Value = origid + count
			else
				print("failed to load clothing asset")
			end
		end
	end
end

getClothingId(shirtid)
getClothingId(pantsid)

		if v.ItemID.Value == id then
			return v
		end
	end
end


function displayWindow(windowname,reason) --reason is optional and only for the errorwarning
	enabled.Value = false
	local window = frame:FindFirstChild(windowname)
	if window then
		if reason ~= nil then
			window.Reason.Text = reason
		end
		window.Visible = true
		repeat wait() until window.Visible == false
	end
	enabled.Value = true
end

function loadSlots(num) --1/4/7/10
	local slot = tonumber(num)
	frame.SelectedPage.Value = slot
	
	for i,v in pairs(outfitslots:GetChildren()) do
		if v.Name ~= "BaseSlot" then
			v:Destroy()
		end
	end
	
	for i=slot,slot+2 do
		local outfit = data.Outfits:FindFirstChild(i)
		if outfit then
			local gui = outfitslots.BaseSlot:clone()
			gui.OutfitName.Text = outfit.OutfitName.Value
			gui.NumberLabel.Text = "#"..i
			gui.Position = gui.Position + UDim2.new((#outfitslots:GetChildren() - 1)*.33,0,0,0)
			gui.Name = i
			gui.Parent = outfitslots
			gui.Visible = true
		end
	end
	setOutfitPageColors()
end

function setOutfitPageColors()
	for _,button in pairs(outfitpages:GetChildren()) do
		button.BackgroundTransparency = .5
		if button.Name ~= "OutfitsPageBaseSlot" then --has outfit page
			button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(140/255, 221/255, 1)
			--button.ItemCost.Text = ""
		end
		if frame.SelectedPage.Value == tonumber(button.Name) then
			button.BackgroundTransparency = .2
		end
	end
end

data.Objects.ChildAdded:connect(function(child)
	if tonumber(child.Name) == outfitpages["4"].ItemID.Value or tonumber(child.Name) == outfitpages["7"].ItemID.Value or tonumber(child.Name) == outfitpages["10"].ItemID.Value then
		setOutfitPageColors()
	end
end)

frame.LoadOutfitPage.Event:connect(function(num)
	loadSlots(num)
end)

frame.ChangeSelectedSlot.Event:connect(function(num)
	frame.SelectedSlot.Value = num
end)

function checkIfSafeToExit() --for loading or closing - (if input boxes are NOT blank, or input boxes do NOT match an already saved outfit)
	local inputsblank = true
	for _,v in pairs(inputs:GetChildren()) do
		if v.Name ~= "RemoveShirt" and v.InputBox.Text ~= "" then
			inputsblank = false
		end
	end
	
	if inputsblank == false then
		for _,outfit in pairs(data.Outfits:GetChildren()) do
			local matching = true
			for _,v in pairs(inputs:GetChildren()) do
				local val = outfit[v.Name].Value
				if val == 0 then
					val = ""
				end
				if v.Name ~= "RemoveShirt" and v.InputBox.Text ~= tostring(val) then
					matching = false
				end
			end
			if matching == true then return true end
		end
		return false
	else
		return true
	end
end

function checkIfSafeToSave() --(if slot is NOT empty, or outfit in that slot is NOT the same)
	local slot = frame.SelectedSlot.Value
	local outfit = data.Outfits:FindFirstChild(slot)
	if outfit then
		if outfit.OutfitName.Value == "" then return true end
		for _,v in pairs(inputs:GetChildren()) do
			if v.Name == "RemoveShirt" then
				if v.Enabled.Value ~= outfit.RemoveShirt.Value then return false end
			else
				local val = tostring(outfit[v.Name].Value)
				if val == 0 then
					val = ""
				end
				if v.InputBox.Text ~= tostring(val) then return false end
			end
		end
		return true
	end
end

--"confirmed" - nil means display a window - true means it's okay to do the action

frame.LoadOutfit.Event:connect(function(confirmed) --load the outfit IDs stored in the SelectedSlot
	local check = checkIfSafeToExit()
	if not confirmed and enabled.Value == true and check == false then
		enabled.Value = false
		displayWindow("LoadWarning")
		enabled.Value = true
	elseif confirmed == true or check == true then
		local slot = frame.SelectedSlot.Value
		local outfit = data.Outfits:FindFirstChild(slot)
		if outfit then
			
			for _,v in pairs(inputs:GetChildren()) do
				if v.Name == "RemoveShirt" then
					v.Enabled.Value = outfit.RemoveShirt.Value
				else
					v.InputBox.Text = outfit[v.Name].Value
					if outfit[v.Name].Value == 0 then
						v.InputBox.Text = ""
					end
				end
			end
			
		end
	end
end)

frame.CloseWindow.Event:connect(function(confirmed)
	local check = checkIfSafeToExit()
	if not confirmed and enabled.Value == true and check == false then
		displayWindow("CloseWarning")
	elseif confirmed == true or check == true then
		module.closeWindow(script.Parent)
	end
end)

frame.SaveOutfit.Event:connect(function(confirmed)
	local check = checkIfSafeToSave()
	if not confirmed and enabled.Value == true and check == false then
		displayWindow("SaveWarning")
	elseif confirmed == true or check == true then
		local slot = frame.SelectedSlot.Value
		local outfit = data.Outfits:FindFirstChild(slot)
		if outfit then
			
			--[[for _,v in pairs(inputs:GetChildren()) do --hah, we gotta FE this
				if v.Name == "RemoveShirt" then
					 outfit.RemoveShirt.Value = v.Enabled.Value
				else
					 outfit[v.Name].Value = v.InputBox.Text
				end
			end]]
			
			local tabletosend = {} --["NameOfInput"] = ID
			for _,v in pairs(inputs:GetChildren()) do
				if v.Name == "RemoveShirt" then
					tabletosend[v.Name] = v.Enabled.Value
				else
					tabletosend[v.Name] = v.InputBox.Text
				end
			end
			local request,statuses = game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.SaveOutfit:InvokeServer(tabletosend,slot)
			if request == true then
				for _,v in pairs(inputs:GetChildren()) do
					v.InputBox.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,1)
				end
				frame.LoadOutfitPage:Fire(frame.SelectedPage.Value)
				displayWindow("SuccessWindow","Outfit saved successfully!")
			elseif request == false then
				if statuses ~= nil then
					for _,v in pairs(inputs:GetChildren()) do
						if tostring(statuses[v.Name]):match("invalid") or tostring(statuses[v.Name]):match("admin") then
							v.InputBox.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,.4,.4)
						end
					end
				end
				displayWindow("ErrorWarning","You have entered at least 1 invalid asset ID. Please correct the invalid IDs.")
			else
				displayWindow("ErrorWarning","To avoid straining the server, please wait a few moments to try this again.")
			end
			
		end
	end
end)

				end
			end

		else
			displayWindow("ErrorWarning","Please wait a few more moments before trying this again.")
		end
		enabled.Value = true
	end
end)

function commanumber(num)
	local num = tostring(num)
	local i = 0
	local finalstring = ""
	for n in string.gmatch(num,"%d") do
		finalstring = finalstring..n
		i = i + 1
		if (#num - i) % 3 == 0 and i ~= #num then
			finalstring = finalstring..","
		end
	end
	return finalstring
end

function SetBarOverheadText()
	frame:WaitForChild("ConsecDaysCount").Text = stats.Spins.Value == 0 and "Visit 5 days in a row = free RHS prize spins!" or stats.Spins.Value > 0 and "Congrats! You earned "..stats.Spins.Value.." free prize spin"..(stats.Spins.Value == 1 and "" or "s").."!"
end

function TextTransparencyFade(element, totalframes, fadein)
	coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(function()
		local i = 0
		local transincrement = (1 - element.TextTransparency) / totalframes
		local strokeincrement = (1 - element.TextStrokeTransparency) / totalframes
		local mult = 1
		if fadein then
			mult = -1
			element.TextTransparency = 1
			element.TextStrokeTransparency = 1
		end
		element.Visible = true
		local render = rs.RenderStepped:connect(function()
			if i < totalframes then
				element.TextTransparency = element.TextTransparency + transincrement * mult
				element.TextStrokeTransparency = element.TextStrokeTransparency + strokeincrement * mult
				i = i + 1
			end
		end)
		repeat wait() until i >= totalframes
		if fadein == false then
			element.Visible = false
			element.TextTransparency = 1 - (transincrement * totalframes)
			element.TextStrokeTransparency = 1 - (strokeincrement * totalframes)
		end
		render:disconnect()
	end),element, totalframes, fadein)
end

function ImageFade(element, totalframes, fadein)
	coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(function()
		local i = 0
		local increment = 1 / totalframes
		local mult = 1
		if fadein then
			mult = -1
			element.ImageTransparency = 1
		else
			element.ImageTransparency = 0
		end
		element.Visible = true
		local render = rs.RenderStepped:connect(function()
			if i < totalframes then
				element.ImageTransparency = element.ImageTransparency + increment * mult
				i = i + 1
			end
		end)
		repeat wait() until i >= totalframes
		if fadein == false then
			element.Visible = false
		end
		render:disconnect()
	end),element, totalframes, fadein)
end

function PlaySound(id,timelength,volume,pitch)
	local sound = Instance.new("Sound",script.Parent)
	sound.Name = "LoyaltySound"
	sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://"..id
	sound.Volume = volume or 0.5
	sound.Pitch = pitch or 1
	sound:Play()
	game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(sound,timelength or 3)
end

		local spinning = false
		local cyclerate = 12
		local winningindex
		
		local function CycleSpinner()
			local recentlyusedtable = {}
			local function DupeCheck(i)
				for _,v in pairs(recentlyusedtable) do
					if v == i then
						return true
					end
				end
				return false
			end
			while spinning do
				local indextodisplay
				local winner = false
				if winningindex ~= nil and DupeCheck(winningindex) == false then
					indextodisplay = winningindex
					winner = true
				else
					repeat
						local n = math.random(1,#prizetable)
						if DupeCheck(n) == false then
							indextodisplay = n
						end
					until indextodisplay ~= nil
				end
				table.insert(recentlyusedtable,indextodisplay)
				if #recentlyusedtable > 5 then
					table.remove(recentlyusedtable,1)
				end
				
				ShowSpinPrize(prizetable,indextodisplay,winner)
				
				if winner then
					spinning = false
					if stats.Spins.Value > 0 then
						frame.RespinButton.Visible = true
					end
					return
				end
				
				wait(1/cyclerate)
			end
		end
		
		local function RunSpin()
			winningindex = nil
			frame.RespinButton.Visible = false
			frame.ShopButton.Visible = false
			frame.CloseButton.Visible = false
			cyclerate = 12
			prizetable = game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.FetchSpinPrizeTable:InvokeServer()
			frame.SpinnerFrame.Visible = true
			frame.StopSpinner.Visible = true
			spinning = true
			spawn(CycleSpinner)
			
			local stopcon
			stopcon = frame.StopSpinner.MouseButton1Click:connect(function()
				stopcon:disconnect()
				frame.StopSpinner.Visible = false
				PlaySound(345593426,nil,.8)
				wait(math.random(3,6)/10)
				for i=1,10 do
					wait(.6 + math.random(1,10)/100 - cyclerate / 40)
					cyclerate = cyclerate - 1
				end
				wait(math.random(1,25)/10)
				winningindex = game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.FetchWinningSpinPrize:InvokeServer()
				local respincon
				respincon = frame.RespinButton.MouseButton1Click:connect(function()
					if stats.Spins.Value > 0 and spinning == false then
						respincon:disconnect()
						PlaySound(345593426,nil,.8)
						RunSpin()
					end
				end)
			end)
		end
		
		RunSpin()
	end)
end

function setButtonColors()
	for _,button in pairs(gui.Items:GetChildren()) do
		if button.Name ~= "BaseButton" then
			if data.Objects:FindFirstChild(button.ItemID.Value) then --has object
				button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(.5,1,.5)
			else
				button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,1)
			end
			if gui.SelectedItem.Value == button.ItemID.Value then
				button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,.25)
			end
		end
	end
end

end

	if enabled.Value == true then
		enabled.Value = false
		local oldbg = button.BackgroundColor3
		local oldborder = button.BorderColor3
		button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(.6,.6,.6)
		button.BorderColor3 = Color3.new(.1,.1,.1)
		local result,reason = game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.PurchaseLoyaltyItem:InvokeServer( gui.SelectedItem.Value) --the item ID
		if result == true then
			local sound = Instance.new("Sound",plr.PlayerGui)
			sound.Name = "ShopBuySuccess"
			sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://"..game.ReplicatedStorage.AssetIDs[sound.Name].Value
			sound:Play()
			game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(sound,3)
		else
			displayFailureWindow(result,reason)
		end
		button.BackgroundColor3 = oldbg
		button.BorderColor3 = oldborder

coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(function()
	while script.Parent ~= nil do
		local timedata = game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData
		local day = data:FindFirstChild("LoyaltyStats") and data.LoyaltyStats.PlayerLoginDay.Value or timedata.CurrentRealLifeDay.Value
		local endtime = timedata.HolidayStartTime.Value + (day * 86400)
		local timeleft = endtime - game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value
		local vals = {math.floor(timeleft/3600), math.floor((timeleft%3600)/60), timeleft%60}
		for i,v in pairs(vals) do
			if #tostring(v) == 1 then
				vals[i] = "0"..v
			end
		end
		if timeleft >= 0 then
			gui.NextLoginTime.Text = "Next daily login reward available in: "..vals[1]..":"..vals[2]..":"..vals[3]
		else
			gui.NextLoginTime.Text = "Daily login reward available! Rejoin the game to claim it."
		end
		wait(1)
	end
end))

local plr = game.Players.LocalPlayer
local shopframe = script.Parent:WaitForChild("Frame")
local frame = shopframe:WaitForChild("CarSelectHolder"):WaitForChild("ScrollingFrame")
local enabled = shopframe:WaitForChild("Enabled")
local selectedcar = plr:WaitForChild("SelectedCar")

local backbutton = shopframe:WaitForChild("BackButton")
local forwardbutton = shopframe:WaitForChild("ForwardButton")

local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:WaitForChild(plr.userId)

local module = require(game.ReplicatedStorage.Modules.InterfaceModule)
module.openWindow(script.Parent.Frame)

function getItemDataFromID(id)
	for i,v in pairs(game.ReplicatedStorage.Objects:GetChildren()) do
		if v.ItemID.Value == id then
			return v
		end
	end
end


function displayFailureWindow(result,reason) --reason == amount of cash needed
	if result == false then
		
		local failure = shopframe.FailureWindow
		failure.ProductId.Value = 0
		failure.Reason.Text = reason
		failure.NormalButton.Visible = true
		failure.BuyCashButtons.Visible = false
		failure.Visible = true
		repeat wait() until failure.Visible == false
		
	elseif result == "cash" then
		
		local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)
		local cash = data.cash.Value
		local products = game.ReplicatedStorage.CashProducts:GetChildren()
		local bestprod
		local highestprod
		for i,v in pairs(products) do
			local diff = tonumber(v.Name) - reason
			if diff >= 0 and (bestprod == nil or tonumber(bestprod.Name) > tonumber(v.Name)) then
				bestprod = v
			end
			if highestprod == nil or tonumber(v.Name) > tonumber(highestprod.Name) then
				highestprod = v
			end
		end
		if bestprod == nil then
			bestprod = highestprod
		end
		
		local failure = shopframe.FailureWindow
		failure.Reason.Text = "You don't have enough Cash to buy this item. Would you like to get more Cash?"
		failure.CashAmount.Value = tonumber(bestprod.Name)
		failure.ProductId.Value = bestprod.Value
		failure.BuyCashButtons.GetCash.Text = "Get +"..bestprod.Name.." Cash!"
		failure.NormalButton.Visible = false
		failure.BuyCashButtons.Visible = true
		failure.Visible = true
		repeat wait() until failure.Visible == false
		
	end
end


function setButtonColors()
	for _,button in pairs(frame:GetChildren()) do
		if button.Name ~= "BaseButton" then
			if data.Objects:FindFirstChild(button.ItemID.Value) then --has object
				button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(.5,1,.5)
				button.ItemCost.Text = ""
			else
				button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,1)
			end
			if button.ItemID.Value == 288 then
				button.ItemCost.Text = ""
			end
			if plr.SelectedCar.Value == button.ItemID.Value then
				button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,.25)
			end
		end
	end
end

function setNavigationButtonColors()
	local canvaswidth = frame.CanvasSize.X.Offset
	local canvaspos = frame.CanvasPosition.X
	local framewidth = frame.AbsoluteSize.X
	
	local isat
	
	if canvaspos == 0 then
		isat = 0
	elseif canvaspos >= canvaswidth - framewidth then
		isat = 1
	end
	
	if isat == 0 then
		backbutton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(107, 120, 245)
		backbutton.ImageTransparency = .7
	else
		backbutton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(128, 244, 120)
		backbutton.ImageTransparency = 0
	end
	
	if isat == 1 then
		forwardbutton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(107, 120, 245)
		forwardbutton.ImageTransparency = .7
	else
		forwardbutton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(128, 244, 120)
		forwardbutton.ImageTransparency = 0
	end
end

shopframe.LoadMenu.Event:connect(function() --create all buttons
	--first, clear any existing buttons
	shopframe:WaitForChild("BuyButton").Visible = false
	shopframe:WaitForChild("ItemDesc").Text = ""
	for i,v in pairs(frame:GetChildren()) do
		if v.Name ~= "BaseButton" then
			v:Destroy()
		end
	end
	frame.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0,0,0,0)
	
	local objectstable = {}
	
	local objdata = game.ReplicatedStorage.Objects
	for _,v in pairs(objdata:GetChildren()) do
		if v.Name:match("vehic_") and not (v:FindFirstChild("OldSchoolCar")) and not (v:FindFirstChild("NotForSale") and data.Objects:FindFirstChild(v.ItemID.Value) == nil) and not ((v:FindFirstChild("ReleaseTime") and game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value - v.ReleaseTime.Value < 0) and game.PlaceId == 9689581) and not (v:FindFirstChild("TimeToGoOffSale") and v.TimeToGoOffSale.Value < game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value and data.Objects:FindFirstChild(v.ItemID.Value) == nil) then
			table.insert(objectstable,v)
		end
	end
	
	local function sortfunc(v1, v2)
		if (v1:FindFirstChild("ReleaseTime") and game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value - v1.ReleaseTime.Value < 86400*24) or (v2:FindFirstChild("ReleaseTime") and game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value - v2.ReleaseTime.Value < 86400*24) then
			if v1:FindFirstChild("ReleaseTime") and game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value - v1.ReleaseTime.Value < 86400*24 and (v2:FindFirstChild("ReleaseTime") == nil or game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value - v2.ReleaseTime.Value > 86400*24) then
				return true
			elseif v2:FindFirstChild("ReleaseTime") and game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value - v2.ReleaseTime.Value < 86400*24 and (v1:FindFirstChild("ReleaseTime") == nil or game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value - v1.ReleaseTime.Value > 86400*24) then
				return false
			elseif v1:FindFirstChild("ReleaseTime") and game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value - v1.ReleaseTime.Value < 86400*24 and v2:FindFirstChild("ReleaseTime") and game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value - v2.ReleaseTime.Value < 86400*24 then
				if v1.ReleaseTime.Value > v2.ReleaseTime.Value then
					return true
				elseif v1.ReleaseTime.Value < v2.ReleaseTime.Value then
					return false
				else
					if v1.ItemID.Value > v2.ItemID.Value then
						return true
					else
						return false
					end
				end
			end
		end
		
		if v1.ItemCost.Value < v2.ItemCost.Value then
			return true
		else
			return false
		end
	end
	
	table.sort(objectstable,sortfunc)
	
	for _,v in pairs(objectstable) do
		local button = frame.BaseButton:clone()
		button.Image = "rbxassetid://"..v.DecalID.Value
		button.ItemID.Value = v.ItemID.Value
		if not data.Objects:FindFirstChild(v.ItemID.Value) then --show cost
			button.ItemCost.Text = "$"..v.ItemCost.Value
		end
		button.Position = UDim2.new(0,(#frame:GetChildren() - 1) * 100,0,0)
		button.Name = string.sub(v.Name,7)
		button.Parent = frame
		if (v:FindFirstChild("ReleaseTime") and game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value - v.ReleaseTime.Value < 86400*24) or v:FindFirstChild("TimeToGoOffSale") then
			if v:FindFirstChild("TimeToGoOffSale") then
				button.LimitedBanner.Visible = true
				local offsaletime = Instance.new("IntValue")
				offsaletime.Name = "OffSaleTime"
				offsaletime.Value = v.TimeToGoOffSale.Value
				offsaletime.Parent = button
			else
				button.NewBanner.Visible = true
			end
			
			for _,x in pairs(frame:GetChildren()) do
				if x.Name ~= "BaseButton" then
					if x ~= button then
						x.Position = x.Position + UDim2.new(0,100,0,0)
					end
				end
			end
			button.Position = UDim2.new(0,0,0,0)
		end
		if v:FindFirstChild("TwitterCode") then
			local icon = script.TwitterBird:clone()
			icon.Parent = button
			icon.Visible = true
			for _,x in pairs(frame:GetChildren()) do
				if x.Name ~= "BaseButton" then
					if x ~= button then
						x.Position = x.Position + UDim2.new(0,100,0,0)
					end
				end
			end
			button.Position = UDim2.new(0,0,0,0)
		end
			
		button.Visible = true
	end
	frame.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0,(#frame:GetChildren() - 1) * 100,0,0)
	
	local id = selectedcar.Value
	local item = getItemDataFromID(id)
	if item then
		if data.Objects:FindFirstChild(id) then
			shopframe.BuyButton.Text = "Spawn"
		else
			if not item:FindFirstChild("TwitterCode") then
				shopframe.BuyButton.Text = "Buy Car"
			else
				shopframe.BuyButton.Visible = false
			end
		end
		if item:FindFirstChild("OffSaleTime") then
			local timeleft = item.OffSaleTime.Value - game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value
			local days = math.floor(timeleft / 86400)
			local timeleftstr = (days >= 2 and (days.." days")) or (days == 1 and "1 day") or "less than 1 day"
			shopframe.LimitedTimeNotice.Text = "Off-sale in: "..timeleftstr
		end
		shopframe.BuyButton.Visible = true
		shopframe.ItemDesc.Text = item.ItemDesc.Value
		shopframe.Header.Text = string.sub(item.Name,7)
	end
	setButtonColors()
	setNavigationButtonColors()
end)


shopframe.ChangeSelection.Event:connect(function(button)
	if button ~= nil and enabled.Value == true then
		local id = button.ItemID.Value
		selectedcar.Value = id
		shopframe.Header.Text = button.Name
		local item = getItemDataFromID(id)
		shopframe.ItemDesc.Text = item.ItemDesc.Value
		shopframe.BuyButton.Visible = true
		shopframe.LimitedTimeNotice.Text = ""
		
		if data.Objects:FindFirstChild(id) then
			shopframe.BuyButton.Text = "Spawn"
		else
			if not item:FindFirstChild("TwitterCode") then
				shopframe.BuyButton.Text = "Buy Car"
			else
				shopframe.BuyButton.Visible = false
			end
			if button:FindFirstChild("OffSaleTime") then
				local timeleft = button.OffSaleTime.Value - game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value
				local days = math.floor(timeleft / 86400)
				local timeleftstr = (days >= 2 and (days.." days")) or (days == 1 and "1 day") or "less than 1 day"
				shopframe.LimitedTimeNotice.Text = "Off-sale in: "..timeleftstr
			end
		end
		setButtonColors()
	end
end)


shopframe.MainButtonPress.Event:connect(function(button)
	if enabled.Value == true then
		enabled.Value = false
		local oldbg = button.BackgroundColor3
		local oldborder = button.BorderColor3
		button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(.6,.6,.6)
		button.BorderColor3 = Color3.new(.1,.1,.1)
		
		if data.Objects:FindFirstChild(selectedcar.Value) then --has car, get the spawn tool
			local bp = plr:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
			local char = plr.Character
			
			if bp and char and char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and not char:FindFirstChild("Spawn Car") then
				if bp:FindFirstChild("Spawn Car") then
					char.Humanoid:EquipTool(bp["Spawn Car"])
				else
					local tool = script.Parent["Spawn Car"]:clone()
					tool.Parent = bp
					wait()
					char.Humanoid:EquipTool(tool)
				end
			end
			game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.ChangeSelectedCar:FireServer(selectedcar.Value)
			module.closeWindow(script.Parent)
			return
		else
			local result,reason = game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.PurchasePermanentItem:InvokeServer(selectedcar.Value)
			if result == true then
				local sound = Instance.new("Sound",plr.PlayerGui)
				sound.Name = "ShopBuySuccess"
				sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://"..game.ReplicatedStorage.AssetIDs[sound.Name].Value --better change this in all scripts!
				sound:Play()
				game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(sound,3)
				shopframe.BuyButton.Text = "Spawn"
			else
				displayFailureWindow(result,reason)
			end
		end
		
		
		button.BackgroundColor3 = oldbg
		button.BorderColor3 = oldborder
		enabled.Value = true
	end
end)

function scrollFrameByButton(val)
	frame.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(frame.CanvasPosition.X + (100 * val),0)
end

forwardbutton.MouseButton1Click:connect(function()
	scrollFrameByButton(1)
end)

backbutton.MouseButton1Click:connect(function()
	scrollFrameByButton(-1)
end)

shopframe.LoadMenu:Fire()

frame.Changed:connect(function(property)
	if property == "CanvasPosition" then
		setNavigationButtonColors()
	end
end)w

local plr = game.Players.LocalPlayer
local mouse = plr:GetMouse()
local Tool = script.Parent
local enabled = true

if plr.Character == nil or plr.Character.Parent == nil then
	plr.CharacterAdded:wait()
end

local char = plr.Character
local torso = char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")

function removeMarker()
	if connection then
		connection:disconnect()
		connection = nil
	end
	if marker then
		marker:Destroy()
		marker = nil
	end
end

function createMarker()
	marker = Instance.new("Part")
	marker.Name = "Marker"
	marker.BrickColor = BrickColor.new("Lime green")
	marker.TopSurface = "Smooth"
	marker.BottomSurface = "Smooth"
	marker.FormFactor = "Custom"
	marker.Size = Vector3.new(.2,.2,.2)
	marker.Anchored = true
	marker.CanCollide = false
	
	local mesh = Instance.new("SpecialMesh")
	mesh.MeshType = "Sphere"
	mesh.Scale = Vector3.new(25,7,25)
	mesh.Offset = Vector3.new(0,-2,0)
	mesh.Parent = marker
	
	marker.Parent = Tool
end

script.Parent.Activated:connect(function()
	if enabled then
		enabled = false
		if mouse.Target and mouse.Target.Name == "Road" then
			local request = game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.SpawnCarRequest:InvokeServer(mouse.Hit)
			if request == true then
				if plr.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("SpawnCarGui") then
					plr.PlayerGui.SpawnCarGui:Destroy()
				end
				removeMarker()
				Tool:Destroy()
			end
		end
		wait(.15)
		enabled = true
	end
end)

script.Parent.Equipped:connect(function()
	local clonegui = script.SpawnCarGui:clone()
	clonegui.Parent = plr.PlayerGui
	clonegui.Frame.Visible = true
	
	connection = game:GetService("RunService").RenderStepped:connect(function()
		if not marker then createMarker() end
		
		if mouse and mouse.Hit and mouse.Target then
			marker.CFrame = CFrame.new(mouse.Hit.p) + Vector3.new(0,2,0)
			if (torso.Position - mouse.Hit.p).magnitude > 52 or mouse.Target.Name ~= "Road" then
				marker.BrickColor = BrickColor.new("Really red")
			else
				marker.BrickColor = BrickColor.new("Lime green")
			end
		end
	end)
end)

script.Parent.Unequipped:connect(function()
	if plr.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("SpawnCarGui") then
		plr.PlayerGui.SpawnCarGui:Destroy()
	end
	removeMarker()
end)

		button.BorderColor3 = oldborder
		enabled.Value = true
	end
end)

local timelabel = frame:WaitForChild("LimitedTimeNotice")

function GetTimeString(timeleft)
	if timeleft < 0 then
		return "Ends now!"
	end
	
	local d, h, m, s 
	
	d = math.floor(timeleft / 86400)
	if d == 0 then
		d = ""
	else
		d = d..":"
	end
	
	h = math.floor((timeleft / 3600) % 24)
	if h < 10 then
		if h == 0 then
			if d == "" then
				h = ""
			else
				h = "00:"
			end
		else
			if d == "" then
				h = h..":"
			else
				h = "0"..h..":"
			end
		end
	else
		h = h..":"
	end
	
	m = math.floor((timeleft / 60) % 60)
	if m < 10 then
		if m == 0 then
			if h == "" then
				m = "0:"
			else
				m = "00:"
			end
		else
			if h == "" then
				m = m..":"
			else
				m = "0"..m..":"
			end
		end
	else
		m = m..":"
	end
	
	s = timeleft % 60
	if s < 10 then
		s = "0"..s
	end
	
	return d..h..m..s
end

function SetTimeString()
	local timeleft = (jointime + 86400 * 7) - currenttimeobj.Value
	if timeleft <= 0 then
		timelabel.Text = "Offer about to expire!"
	else
		timelabel.Text = "Offer ends in "..GetTimeString(timeleft)
	end
end

SetTimeString()

currenttimeobj.Changed:connect(function()
	SetTimeString()
end)

function Teleport(destinationpart)
	local tpgui = Instance.new("ScreenGui",script.Parent)
	local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
	frame.Size = UDim2.new(2,0,2,0)
	frame.Position = UDim2.new(-.5,0,-.5,0)
	frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	frame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0,0,0)
	frame.Parent = tpgui
	
	for i=1,7 do
		frame.BackgroundTransparency = frame.BackgroundTransparency - .15
		wait(1/35)
	end
	
	if destinationpart.Parent.Name == "WarpPipeMadi" and destinationpart.Parent.Parent.Parent ~= workspace then
		--load the island into the workspace
		destinationpart.Parent.Parent.Parent = workspace
	end
	
	hrp.CFrame = destinationpart.CFrame + destinationpart.CFrame.lookVector * 10
	hrp.Velocity = Vector3.new(0,0,0)
	hrp.Anchored = true
	
	wait(1.6)
	
	hrp.Anchored = false
		
	for i=1,7 do
		frame.BackgroundTransparency = frame.BackgroundTransparency + .15
		wait(1/35)
	end
	
	tpgui:Destroy()
end
