--[[
	// FileName: TouchJump
	// Version 1.0
	// Written by: jmargh
	// Description: Implements jump controls for touch devices. Use with Thumbstick and Thumbpad
--]]

local Players = game:GetService('Players')
local GuiService = game:GetService('GuiService')

local jumpButtonChangeFlagSuccess, jumpButtonChangeFlagValue = pcall(function() return UserSettings():IsUserFeatureEnabled("UserJumpButtonPositionChange") end)
local repositionJumpButton = (jumpButtonChangeFlagSuccess and jumpButtonChangeFlagValue)

local TouchJump = {}

local MasterControl = require(script.Parent)

--[[ Script Variables ]]--
while not Players.LocalPlayer do
	wait()
end
local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
local Humanoid = MasterControl:GetHumanoid()
local JumpButton = nil
local OnInputEnded = nil		-- defined in Create()
local CharacterAddedConnection = nil
local HumStateConnection = nil
local HumChangeConnection = nil
local ExternallyEnabled = false --[[I don't think there's anything that disables jump from outside this script, but in case that ever happens, 
we don't want to re-enable it when jump power is changed]]
local JumpPower = 0
local JumpStateEnabled = true

--[[ Constants ]]--
local TOUCH_CONTROL_SHEET = "rbxasset://textures/ui/TouchControlsSheet.png"

--[[ Private Functions ]]--

local function updateEnabled()
	if JumpPower > 0 and JumpStateEnabled then
		TouchJump:Enable()
	else
		TouchJump:Disable()
	end
end

local function humanoidChanged(prop)
	if prop == "JumpPower" then
		JumpPower =  Humanoid.JumpPower
		updateEnabled()
	elseif prop == "Parent" then
		if not Humanoid.Parent then
			HumChangeConnection:disconnect()
		end
	end
end

local function humandoidStateEnabledChanged(state, isEnabled)
	if state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping then
		JumpStateEnabled = isEnabled
		updateEnabled()
	end
end

local function disableButton()
	JumpButton.Visible = false
	OnInputEnded()
end

local function enableButton()
	if Humanoid and ExternallyEnabled then
		if Humanoid.JumpPower > 0 then
			JumpButton.Visible = true
		end
	end
end

local function characterAdded(newCharacter)
	if HumChangeConnection then
		HumChangeConnection:disconnect()
	end
	-- rebind event to new Humanoid
	Humanoid = nil
	repeat
		Humanoid = MasterControl:GetHumanoid()
		wait()
	until Humanoid and Humanoid.Parent == newCharacter
	HumChangeConnection = Humanoid.Changed:connect(humanoidChanged)
	HumStateConnection = Humanoid.StateEnabledChanged:connect(humandoidStateEnabledChanged)
	JumpPower = Humanoid.JumpPower
	JumpStateEnabled = Humanoid:GetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
	updateEnabled()
end

local function setupCharacterAddedFunction()
	CharacterAddedConnection = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:connect(characterAdded)
	if LocalPlayer.Character then
		characterAdded(LocalPlayer.Character)
	end
end

--[[ Public API ]]--
function TouchJump:Enable()
	ExternallyEnabled = true
	enableButton()
end

function TouchJump:Disable()
	ExternallyEnabled = false
	disableButton()
end

function TouchJump:Create(parentFrame)
	if JumpButton then
		JumpButton:Destroy()
		JumpButton = nil
	end
	
	local isSmallScreen = parentFrame.AbsoluteSize.y <= 500
	local jumpButtonSize = isSmallScreen and 70 or 120
	
	JumpButton = Instance.new('ImageButton')
	JumpButton.Name = "JumpButton"
	JumpButton.Visible = false
	JumpButton.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	JumpButton.Image = TOUCH_CONTROL_SHEET
	JumpButton.ImageRectOffset = Vector2.new(176, 222)
	JumpButton.ImageRectSize = Vector2.new(174, 174)
	JumpButton.Size = UDim2.new(0, jumpButtonSize, 0, jumpButtonSize)
	
	if not repositionJumpButton then
		JumpButton.Position = isSmallScreen and UDim2.new(1, jumpButtonSize * -2.25, 1, -jumpButtonSize - 20) or
			UDim2.new(1, jumpButtonSize * -2.75, 1, -jumpButtonSize - 120)
	else
		JumpButton.Position = isSmallScreen and UDim2.new(1, -(jumpButtonSize*1.5-10), 1, -jumpButtonSize - 20) or
			UDim2.new(1, -(jumpButtonSize*1.5-10), 1, -jumpButtonSize * 1.75)
	end
	
	local touchObject = nil	
	JumpButton.InputBegan:connect(function(inputObject)
		if touchObject or inputObject.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			return
		end
		
		touchObject = inputObject
		JumpButton.ImageRectOffset = Vector2.new(0, 222)
		MasterControl:SetIsJumping(true)
	end)
	
	OnInputEnded = function()
		touchObject = nil
		MasterControl:SetIsJumping(false)
		JumpButton.ImageRectOffset = Vector2.new(176, 222)
	end
	
	JumpButton.InputEnded:connect(function(inputObject)
		if inputObject == touchObject then
			OnInputEnded()
		end
	end)
	
	GuiService.MenuOpened:connect(function()
		if touchObject then
			OnInputEnded()
		end
	end)
	
	if not CharacterAddedConnection then
		setupCharacterAddedFunction()
	end
	
	JumpButton.Parent = parentFrame
end

return TouchJump

--Made by Luckymaxer

BaseUrl = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id="

RbxUtility = LoadLibrary("RbxUtility")
Create = RbxUtility.Create

BasePart = Create("Part"){
	Material = Enum.Material.Plastic,
	Shape = Enum.PartType.Block,
	TopSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth,
	BottomSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth,
	FormFactor = Enum.FormFactor.Custom,
	Size = Vector3.new(0.2, 0.2, 0.2),
	Anchored = false,
	CanCollide = true,
	Locked = true
}

MeshData = {
	Meshes = {
		Body = 112511429,
	},
	TextureId = 104515809
}

function CreateVehicle()

	local ExhaustSmoke = {}
	local Lights = {}
	local Sparkles = {}

	local Body = BasePart:Clone()
	Body.Name = "Body"
	Body.Size = Vector3.new(1, 4.9, 6)
	local BodyMesh = Create("SpecialMesh"){
		MeshType = Enum.MeshType.FileMesh,
		MeshId = MeshData.Meshes.Body,
		TextureId = MeshData.TextureId,
		Scale = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1),
		VertexColor = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1),
		Offset = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0),
		Parent = Body
	}
	
	local BaseSmokePart = BasePart:Clone()
	BaseSmokePart.Name = "SmokePart"
	BaseSmokePart.Transparency = 1
	local BaseExhaustSmoke = Create("Smoke"){
		Name = "ExhaustSmoke",
		Size = 0.1,
		RiseVelocity = 0.01,
		Color = Color3.new((127 / 255), (127 / 255), (127 / 255)),
		Enabled = true,
		Parent = BaseSmokePart,
	}

	local BaseLightPart = BasePart:Clone()
	BaseLightPart.Name = "LightPart"
	BaseLightPart.Transparency = 1
	local Light = Create("PointLight"){
		Name = "Light",
		Brightness = 50,
		Color = Color3.new((255 / 255), (252 / 255), (153 / 255)),
		Range = 10,
		Shadows = false,
		Enabled = false,
		Parent = BaseLightPart,
	}
	
	local ExhaustPipe = BaseSmokePart:Clone()
	ExhaustPipe.Name = "ExhaustPipe"
	ExhaustPipe.Parent = Body
	table.insert(ExhaustSmoke, ExhaustPipe.ExhaustSmoke)
	
	local SmokeWeld = Create("Weld"){
		C0 = CFrame.new(0, 1.45, -2.75) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, 0),
		Part0 = ExhaustPipe,
		Part1 = Body,
		Parent = ExhaustPipe
	}

	local Tables = {
		ExhaustSmoke = ExhaustSmoke,
		Lights = Lights,
		Sparkles = Sparkles
	}
	
	return {
		Vehicle = Body,
		Tables = Tables
	}
	
end

for i, v in pairs(MeshData) do
	if type(v) == "table" then
		for ii, vv in pairs(v) do
			if type(vv) == "string" or type(vv) == "number" then
				MeshData[i][ii] = (BaseUrl .. tostring(vv))
			end
		end
	elseif type(v) == "string" or type(v) == "number" then
		MeshData[i] = (BaseUrl .. tostring(v))
	end
end

return {
	BaseUrl = BaseUrl,
	MeshData = MeshData,
	CreateVehicle = CreateVehicle
}

BaseUrl = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id="

RbxUtility = LoadLibrary("RbxUtility")
Create = RbxUtility.Create

BasePart = Create("Part"){
	Material = Enum.Material.Plastic,
	Shape = Enum.PartType.Block,
	TopSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth,
	BottomSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth,
	FormFactor = Enum.FormFactor.Custom,
	Size = Vector3.new(0.2, 0.2, 0.2),
	Anchored = false,
	CanCollide = true,
	Locked = true
}

	Meshes = {
		Body = 112511429,

	TextureId = 112511290

		Enabled = false,

	for i, v in pairs(HeadLight:GetChildren()) do
		if v:IsA("Sparkles") then
			table.insert(Sparkles, v)
		end

function CreateVehicle()

	local Lights = {}
	local Sparkles = {}

	Body.Name = "Body"
	Body.Size = Vector3.new(1, 3.625, 8)
	local BodyMesh = Create("SpecialMesh"){
		MeshType = Enum.MeshType.FileMesh,
		MeshId = MeshData.Meshes.Body,
		TextureId = MeshData.TextureId,
		Scale = Vector3.new(1.25, 1.25, 1.25),
		VertexColor = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1),
		Offset = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0),
		Parent = Body
	}
	
	local BaseWheel = BasePart:Clone()
	BaseWheel.Size = Vector3.new(0.5, 1, 1)
	BaseWheel.CanCollide = false
	local WheelMesh = Create("SpecialMesh"){
		MeshType = Enum.MeshType.FileMesh,
		MeshId = "",
		TextureId = MeshData.TextureId,
		Scale = Vector3.new(1.25, 1.25, 1.25),
		VertexColor = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1),
		Offset = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0),
		Parent = BaseWheel,
	}

	local BaseFrontWheel = BaseWheel:Clone()
	BaseFrontWheel.Name = "FrontWheel"
	BaseFrontWheel.Mesh.MeshId = MeshData.Meshes.Wheel
	
	local BaseBackWheel = BaseWheel:Clone()
	BaseBackWheel.Name = "BackWheel"
	BaseBackWheel.Mesh.MeshId = MeshData.Meshes.Wheel

	local BaseSmokePart = BasePart:Clone()
	BaseSmokePart.Name = "SmokePart"
	BaseSmokePart.Transparency = 1
	local BaseExhaustSmoke = Create("Smoke"){
		Name = "ExhaustSmoke",
		Size = 0.1,
		RiseVelocity = 0.01,
		Color = Color3.new((127 / 255), (127 / 255), (127 / 255)),
		Enabled = true,
		Parent = BaseSmokePart,
	}

	local BaseLightPart = BasePart:Clone()
	BaseLightPart.Name = "LightPart"
	BaseLightPart.Transparency = 1
	local Light = Create("SpotLight"){
		Name = "Light",
		Brightness = 50,
		Angle = 45,
		Color = Color3.new((40 / 255), (127 / 255), (71 / 255)),
		Range = 20,
		Shadows = false,
		Enabled = false,
		Parent = BaseLightPart,
	}
			
	local FrontWheel = BaseFrontWheel:Clone()
	FrontWheel.Parent = Body
	
	local FrontMotor = Create("Motor6D"){
		Name = "FrontMotor",
		Part0 = Body,
		Part1 = FrontWheel,
		C0 = CFrame.new(0, -0.5, -3.375) * CFrame.Angles(0, (math.pi / 2), 0),
		C1 = CFrame.new() * CFrame.Angles(0, -(math.pi / 2), 0),
		Parent = Body
	}

	BackWheel.Parent = Body

		Part0 = Body,
		Part1 = BackWheel,
		C0 = CFrame.new(0, -0.5, 3.1) * CFrame.Angles(0, (math.pi / 2), 0),
		C1 = CFrame.new() * CFrame.Angles(0, -(math.pi / 2), 0),
		Parent = Body
	}
	
	local HeadLight = BaseLightPart:Clone()
	HeadLight.Parent = Body
	table.insert(Lights, HeadLight.Light)
	for i, v in pairs(HeadLight:GetChildren()) do
		if v:IsA("Sparkles") then
			table.insert(Sparkles, v)
		end
	end
	
	local LightWeld = Create("Weld"){
		Part0 = Body,
		Part1 = HeadLight,
		C0 = CFrame.new(0, 1.25, -1.9) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, 0),
		Parent = HeadLight
	}

	local Tables = {
		ExhaustSmoke = ExhaustSmoke,
		Lights = Lights,
		Sparkles = Sparkles
	}
	
	return {
		Vehicle = Body,
		Tables = Tables
	}
	
end

for i, v in pairs(MeshData) do
	if type(v) == "table" then
		for ii, vv in pairs(v) do
			if type(vv) == "string" or type(vv) == "number" then
				MeshData[i][ii] = (BaseUrl .. tostring(vv))
			end
		end
	elseif type(v) == "string" or type(v) == "number" then
		MeshData[i] = (BaseUrl .. tostring(v))
	end
end

return {
	BaseUrl = BaseUrl,
	MeshData = MeshData,
	CreateVehicle = CreateVehicle
}

for i, v in pairs(MeshData) do
	if type(v) == "table" then
		for ii, vv in pairs(v) do
			if type(vv) == "string" or type(vv) == "number" then
				MeshData[i][ii] = (BaseUrl .. tostring(vv))
			end
		end
	elseif type(v) == "string" or type(v) == "number" then
		MeshData[i] = (BaseUrl .. tostring(v))
	end
end

return {
	BaseUrl = BaseUrl,
	MeshData = MeshData,
	CreateVehicle = CreateVehicle

MeshData = {
	Meshes = {
		Body = 206414124,
		Wheel = 206414152,
		SideCar = 477750963,
		SideCarWheel = 477751306,
	},
	TextureId = 204410898
}

function CreateVehicle()

	local ExhaustSmoke = {}
	local Lights = {}
	local Sparkles = {}

	local Body = BasePart:Clone()
	Body.Name = "Body"
	Body.Size = Vector3.new(1, 3.625, 8)
	local BodyMesh = Create("SpecialMesh"){
		Name = "Mesh",
		MeshType = Enum.MeshType.FileMesh,
		MeshId = MeshData.Meshes.Body,
		TextureId = MeshData.TextureId,
		Scale = Vector3.new(1.25, 1.25, 1.25),
		VertexColor = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1),
		Offset = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0),
		Parent = Body,
	}
	
	local SideCar = Body:Clone()
	SideCar.Name = "SideCar"
	SideCar.Size = Vector3.new(3, 2.25, 5.5)
	SideCar.Mesh.MeshId = MeshData.Meshes.SideCar
	Create("Weld"){
		Name = "Weld",
		Part0 = Body,
		Part1 = SideCar,
		C0 = CFrame.new(-2.375, -0.125, 1.225),
		C1 = CFrame.new(0, 0, 0),
		Parent = SideCar,
	}
	SideCar.Parent = Body
	
	local BaseWheel = BasePart:Clone()
	BaseWheel.Size = Vector3.new(0.5, 1, 1)
	BaseWheel.CanCollide = false
	local WheelMesh = Create("SpecialMesh"){
		Name = "Mesh",
		MeshType = Enum.MeshType.FileMesh,
		MeshId = "",
		TextureId = MeshData.TextureId,
		Scale = Vector3.new(1.25, 1.25, 1.25),
		VertexColor = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1),
		Offset = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0),
		Parent = BaseWheel,
	}

	local BaseFrontWheel = BaseWheel:Clone()
	BaseFrontWheel.Name = "FrontWheel"
	BaseFrontWheel.Mesh.MeshId = MeshData.Meshes.Wheel
	
	local BaseBackWheel = BaseWheel:Clone()
	BaseBackWheel.Name = "BackWheel"
	BaseBackWheel.Mesh.MeshId = MeshData.Meshes.Wheel
	
	local BaseSideCarWheel = BaseWheel:Clone()
	BaseSideCarWheel.Name = "Wheel"
	BaseSideCarWheel.Mesh.MeshId = MeshData.Meshes.SideCarWheel

	local BaseSmokePart = BasePart:Clone()
	BaseSmokePart.Name = "SmokePart"
	BaseSmokePart.Transparency = 1
	local BaseExhaustSmoke = Create("Smoke"){
		Name = "ExhaustSmoke",
		Size = 0.1,
		RiseVelocity = 0.01,
		Color = Color3.new((127 / 255), (127 / 255), (127 / 255)),
		Enabled = true,
		Parent = BaseSmokePart,
	}

	local BaseLightPart = BasePart:Clone()
	BaseLightPart.Name = "LightPart"
	BaseLightPart.Transparency = 1
	local Light = Create("SpotLight"){
		Name = "Light",
		Brightness = 50,
		Angle = 45,
		Color = Color3.new((40 / 255), (127 / 255), (71 / 255)),
		Range = 20,
		Shadows = false,
		Enabled = false,
		Parent = BaseLightPart,
	}

		Name = "FrontMotor",
		Part0 = Body,
		Part1 = FrontWheel,
		C0 = CFrame.new(0, -0.5, -3.375) * CFrame.Angles(0, (math.pi / 2), 0),
		C1 = CFrame.new() * CFrame.Angles(0, -(math.pi / 2), 0),
		Parent = Body

	BackWheel.Parent = Body

=== chunk=479 type=PROP offset=161121 runSha256=b5d3527a892d91aa7541ed6b3c383bedd221a94252103460b71a157e24da3dd8 ===
--Made by Luckymaxer

Tool = script.Parent

Players = game:GetService("Players")
Debris = game:GetService("Debris")

RbxUtility = LoadLibrary("RbxUtility")
Create = RbxUtility.Create

BasePart = Create("Part"){
	Material = Enum.Material.Plastic,
	TopSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth,
	BottomSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth,
	FormFactor = Enum.FormFactor.Custom,
	TopSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.SmoothNoOutlines,
	BottomSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.SmoothNoOutlines,
	FrontSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.SmoothNoOutlines,
	BackSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.SmoothNoOutlines,
	LeftSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.SmoothNoOutlines,
	RightSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.SmoothNoOutlines,
	Locked = true,
	CanCollide = true,
	Anchored = false,
}

LightningRadius = 60

Rate = (1 / 60)

function IsTeamMate(Player1, Player2)
	return (Player1 and Player2 and not Player1.Neutral and not Player2.Neutral and Player1.TeamColor == Player2.TeamColor)
end

function GetTotalParts(MaxParts, PossibleParts, Parts)
	if MaxParts < PossibleParts then
		return MaxParts
	elseif Parts >= MaxParts then
		return 0
	elseif MaxParts >= PossibleParts then
		local PartCount = (MaxParts - PossibleParts)
		if Parts <= MaxParts then
			PartCount = (MaxParts - Parts)
			if PartCount > PossibleParts then
				return PossibleParts
			else
				return PartCount
			end
		elseif PartCount >= PossibleParts then
			return PossibleParts
		else
			return PartCount
		end
	end
end

function GetParts(Region, MaxParts, Ignore)
	local Parts = {}
	local RerunFailed = false
	while #Parts < MaxParts and not RerunFailed do
		local Region = Region
		local PossibleParts = GetTotalParts(MaxParts, 100, #Parts)
		local PartsNearby = game:GetService("Workspace"):FindPartsInRegion3WithIgnoreList(Region, Ignore, PossibleParts)
		if #PartsNearby == 0 then
			RerunFailed = true
		else
			for i, v in pairs(PartsNearby) do
				table.insert(Parts, v)
				table.insert(Ignore, v)
			end
		end
	end
	return Parts
end

function CheckTableForInstance(Table, Instance)
	for i, v in pairs(Table) do
		if v == Instance then
			return true
		end
	end
	return false
end

function GetPlayersNearby(Player, Position, Radius)
	local NegativeRegion = (Position - Vector3.new(Radius, Radius, Radius))
	local PositiveRegion = (Position + Vector3.new(Radius, Radius, Radius))
	local Region = Region3.new(NegativeRegion, PositiveRegion)
	local IgnoreList = {((Player and Player.Character) or nil)}
	for i, v in pairs(Players:GetChildren()) do
		if v:IsA("Player") and v ~= Player and v.Character and v.Character.Parent and IsTeamMate(Player, v) then
			table.insert(IgnoreList, v.Character)
		end
	end
	local Parts = GetParts(Region, 500, IgnoreList)
	local Humanoids = {}
	for i, v in pairs(Parts) do
		local character = v.Parent
		if character:IsA("Hat") then
			character = character.Parent
		end
		local humanoid = character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
		local torso = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0 and torso and not CheckTableForInstance(Humanoids, humanoid) then
			table.insert(Humanoids, humanoid)
		end
	end
	return Humanoids
end

function GetLightning()

	local function LightningStrike(Source, Destination)
		local MaxSegLength = 12
		local MinSegLength = 4
		local MaxOffset = 4
		local Direction = (Destination - Source).unit
		local Distance = (Source - Destination).magnitude
		
		local Points = {}	
		Points[1] = Source
		local LastDist = 0
		
		while (Distance - LastDist) > MaxSegLength do
			LastDist = (LastDist + ((math.random() * (MaxSegLength - MinSegLength)) + MinSegLength))
			for i = 1, 2 do
				local NewVector = (Source + (LastDist * Direction) + Vector3.new(math.random(), math.random(), math.random()))
				table.insert(Points, NewVector)
			end
			wait(Rate)
		end	
		table.insert(Points, Destination)
		
		local Parts = {}
		
		local LightningModel = Create("Model"){
			Name = "LightningModel",
		}
		Debris:AddItem(LightningModel, 3)
		LightningModel.Parent = game:GetService("Workspace")	
		
		local Color = BrickColor.new("Really blue")
		for i, v in pairs(Points) do
			local LastPart = BasePart:Clone()
			LastPart.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
			LastPart.BrickColor = Color
			LastPart.Transparency = 0.3
			LastPart.Anchored = true
			LastPart.CanCollide = false
			for i, v in pairs({"Front", "Back", "Left", "Right", "Top", "Bottom"}) do
				local Light = Instance.new("SurfaceLight")
				Light.Face = Enum.NormalId[v]
				Light.Range = 4
				Light.Brightness = 100
				Light.Color = LastPart.BrickColor.Color
				Light.Enabled = true
				Light.Parent = LastPart
			end
			local Mesh = Create("CylinderMesh"){
				Scale = Vector3.new(0.5, 1, 0.5),
				Parent = LastPart,
			}
			if Points[i + 1] then		
				LastPart.Size = Vector3.new(0.5, (v - Points[i + 1]).magnitude, 0.5)
				LastPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(((v + Points[i + 1]) / 2), Points[i + 1]) * CFrame.Angles((math.pi / 2), 0, 0)
				Debris:AddItem(LastPart, 1)
				LastPart.Parent = LightningModel
				wait(Rate)
			end
		end
	end
	
	local function DoLightning(Position, HitPlayers, Player)
		local humanoids = GetPlayersNearby(Player, Position, LightningRadius)
		for i, v in pairs(humanoids) do
			local character = v.Parent
			local torso = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			if v and v.Health > 0 and torso then
				Spawn(function()
					LightningStrike(Position, torso.Position)
				end)
				HitPlayers[character] = true
				v:TakeDamage(100)
				DoLightning(Position, HitPlayers, Player)
				return true
			end
		end
		return false
	end
	
	local function Strike(Table)
		if not Table then
			return
		end
		local Position = Table.Position
		local Player = Table.Player
		if not Position or not Player then
			return
		end
		local HitPlayers = {}
		--if not DoLightning(Position, HitPlayers, Player) then
			for i = 1, math.random(8, 25), 1 do	
				Spawn(function()
					LightningStrike(Position, (Position + (Vector3.new(((math.random() - 0.5) * 2), ((math.random() - 0.5) * 2),((math.random() - 0.5) * 2)) * 45)))
				end)
			end
		--end
	end
	
	return {
		LightningStrike = LightningStrike,
		DoLightning = DoLightning,
		Strike = Strike,
	}

end

return GetLightning

=== chunk=479 type=PROP offset=175049 runSha256=d95d9ed47c0020d3bc76c315c281d0806f2f711544e29d59c285d9f1dbd27c0f ===
local function absVector(vector)
        return Vector3.new(math.abs(vector.X), math.abs(vector.Y), math.abs(vector.Z))
end
 
local function size(obj)
        return obj and (obj:IsA("BasePart") and obj.Size or obj:IsA("Model") and obj:GetModelSize())
end
 
local function cframe(obj)
        return obj and (obj:IsA("BasePart") and obj.CFrame or obj:IsA("Model") and obj:GetModelCFrame())
end
 
 
function getAllEdges()
        local edges = {}
       
        for _,axis in next,Enum.Axis:GetEnumItems() do
                local faces = {Vector3.FromAxis(axis), -Vector3.FromAxis(axis)}
                               
                for _,face in next,faces do
                        for _,normalId in next,Enum.NormalId:GetEnumItems() do
                                normalId = Vector3.FromNormalId(normalId)
                                if normalId ~= faces[1] and normalId ~= faces[2] then
                                        edges[tostring(face+normalId)] = {face, normalId}
                                end
                        end
                end
        end
       
        for index,edgeData in next,edges do
                table.insert(edges, edgeData)
                edges[index] = nil
        end
       
        return edges
end
 
function getEdgeData(object, edges, thickness)
        for index,edgeData in next,edges do
                local face1,face2 = unpack(edgeData)
                local combined = face1+face2
                local unused = Vector3.new(1,1,1) - absVector(combined)
               
                local edgeSize = (size(object) * unused) + (Vector3.new(1,1,1) * thickness)
                local edgeCFrame = cframe(object) * CFrame.new((size(object) / 2) * combined)
               
                edges[index] = {edgeCFrame, edgeSize}
        end
       
        return edges
end
 
function getAllVerticies(part)
        local verticies = {}
        local isWedge = (part:FindFirstChild("Mesh") and part.Mesh:IsA("SpecialMesh") and part.Mesh.MeshType == Enum.MeshType.Wedge) or part:IsA("WedgePart")
 
        for index,edgeData in next,getAllEdges() do
                local face1,face2 = unpack(edgeData)
                local combined = face1+face2
                local unused = Vector3.new(1,1,1) - absVector(combined)
                if not isWedge then
                        table.insert(verticies, combined + unused)
                        table.insert(verticies, combined - unused)
                else
                        if combined.Z ~= -1 and combined.Y ~= 1 then
                                        table.insert(verticies, combined + unused)
                                        table.insert(verticies, combined - unused)
                        end
                end
        end
       
        return verticies
end
 
return function(model)
        assert(model:IsA("Model"))
        local maxX, minX, maxY, minY, maxZ, minZ = -math.huge,math.huge,-math.huge,math.huge,-math.huge,math.huge
        for i,v in next,model:GetChildren() do
			if v:IsA("BasePart") then
                local verticies = getAllVerticies(v)
                for _,vertex in next,verticies do
                        local meshScale = Vector3.new(1,1,1)
                        local vertexOffset = vertex*v.Size*meshScale/2;
                        local vertexCFrame = v.CFrame:toWorldSpace(CFrame.new(vertexOffset));
                        maxX = math.max(maxX, vertexCFrame.X)
                        minX = math.min(minX, vertexCFrame.X)
                        maxY = math.max(maxY, vertexCFrame.Y)
                        minY = math.min(minY, vertexCFrame.Y)
                        maxZ = math.max(maxZ, vertexCFrame.Z)
                        minZ = math.min(minZ, vertexCFrame.Z)
                end
			end
        end
       
        local size = Vector3.new(maxX-minX, maxY-minY, maxZ-minZ)
        local cframe = CFrame.new(Vector3.new(maxX+minX, maxY+minY, maxZ+minZ)/2)
        return size,cframe
end

local module = {}

function FindButtonInGui(instance)
	local button
	local function recurse(instance)
		for _,v in pairs(instance:GetChildren()) do
			if v:IsA("GuiButton") and v.Visible and v.Name ~= "CloseButton" then
				button = v
				return
			else
				if (v:IsA("GuiButton") or v:IsA("Frame") or v:IsA("ScrollingFrame")) and v.Visible then
					recurse(v)
				end
			end
		end
	end
	
	recurse(instance)
	return button
end

module.SetGamepadSelection = function(gui,instant)
	local frame
	for _,v in pairs(gui:GetChildren()) do
		if v:IsA("Frame") or v:IsA("ScrollingFrame") then
			frame = v
		end
	end
	if frame then
		if frame.Visible == false then return end
		if not instant then
			repeat wait(1/30) until gui == nil or frame == nil or frame.Visible
			if not gui or not frame then return end
			if gui and gui.Name == "ACTIVE_BuyClothing" then
				repeat wait(1/30) until gui == nil or frame == nil or frame:FindFirstChild("Pants") or frame:FindFirstChild("Shirt")
			end
		end
		if gui and frame then
			local closebutton = frame:FindFirstChild("CloseButton")
			local button
			local itemsframe = frame:FindFirstChild("Items")
			if itemsframe then
				frame = itemsframe
			end
			for _,v in pairs(frame:GetChildren()) do
				if v:IsA("GuiButton") and v.Name ~= "CloseButton" and v.Visible then
					button = v
					break
				end
			end
			if button then
				game:GetService("GuiService").SelectedObject = button
			else
				button = FindButtonInGui(frame)
				if button then
					game:GetService("GuiService").SelectedObject = button
				end
			end
			
			if button == nil and closebutton then
				game:GetService("GuiService").SelectedObject = closebutton
			end
		end
	end
end

return module

local module = {}

module.canvases = {}

local msgcooldown = {}

local maxpaint = 250

local isevent = true --turn this off after event

function AddToCooldown(plr)
	coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(function()
		table.insert(msgcooldown,plr.Name)
		wait(3)
		table.remove(msgcooldown,1)
	end))
end

module.SetCanvases = function()
	local tempcanvases = {}
	for _,v in pairs(workspace.CanvasPapers:GetChildren()) do
		if v.Name == "!Canvas" then
			table.insert(tempcanvases,v)
		end
		module.canvases = tempcanvases
	end
end

function CheckCooldown(plr)
	for _,v in pairs(msgcooldown) do
		if plr.Name == v then
			return false
		end
	end
	return true
end

function Notification(plr,msg)
	if CheckCooldown(plr) then
		if game:GetService("RunService"):IsServer() then
			game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireClient(plr, msg)
		else
			game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.LocalNotification:Fire(msg)
		end
		AddToCooldown(plr)
	end
end

--[[module.CheckForEventWin = function(plr)
	if isevent then

		local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)

					game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.EventPrizeAwardedToPlayer:FireClient(plr, "Painting")
				end))
			end
		end
	end
end]]

function ApplyPaint(plr, paintmodel, canvas, position, mode, brickcolor)
	local part = Instance.new("Part")
	part.Name = "ServerPart"
	if game:GetService("RunService"):IsClient() then
		part.Name = "LocalPart"
	end
	part.Shape = "Ball"
	part.Size = Vector3.new(.3,.3,.3)
	part.CFrame = CFrame.new(position)
	part.Anchored = true
	
	if mode == "Paint" then
		part.BrickColor = brickcolor or BrickColor.Red()
		part.TopSurface = "Smooth"
		part.BottomSurface = "Smooth"
		part.Material = "SmoothPlastic"
	else
		part.Transparency = 1
	end
	
	if mode == "Erase" then
		for _,v in pairs(paintmodel:GetChildren()) do
			if v:IsA("Part") then
				v.CanCollide = true
			end
		end
	end
	
	part.Parent = paintmodel
	
	local touchingparts = part:GetTouchingParts()
	
	part.Parent = paintmodel
	
	if mode == "Erase" then
		for _,v in pairs(paintmodel:GetChildren()) do
			if v:IsA("Part") then
				v.CanCollide = false
			end
		end
	end
	
	part.CanCollide = false
	
	if mode == "Paint" then
		local istouching = false
		for _,v in pairs(touchingparts) do
			if v == canvas then
				istouching = true
			end
		end
		if not istouching then part:Destroy() return end
		
		if game:GetService("RunService"):IsClient() then
			local request = game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.PlacePaintRequest:InvokeServer(canvas, position, mode, brickcolor)
			if part then
				part:Destroy()
			end
		end
		
		return true
	elseif mode == "Erase" then
		for _,v in pairs(touchingparts) do
			if v.Parent == paintmodel then
				v:Destroy()
			end
		end
		
		part:Destroy()
		
		if #paintmodel:GetChildren() == 0 then
			canvas.CanvasOwner.Value = nil
		end
		
		if game:GetService("RunService"):IsClient() then
			local request = game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.PlacePaintRequest:InvokeServer(canvas, position, mode)
		end
		
		return true
	end
	
end

module.GetMaxPaint = function()
	return maxpaint
end

module.EraseAllPaint = function(plr)
	local paintmodel = game.ReplicatedStorage.ArtCanvases:FindFirstChild(plr.Name) or workspace.ArtCanvases:FindFirstChild(plr.Name)
	--[[if game:GetService("RunService"):IsServer() then
		module.CheckForEventWin(plr)
	end]]
	if paintmodel then
		for _,v in pairs(paintmodel:GetChildren()) do
			v:Destroy()
		end
	end
	for _,v in pairs(module.canvases) do
		if v.CanvasOwner.Value == plr then
			v.CanvasOwner.Value = nil
		end
	end
	if game:GetService("RunService"):IsClient() then
		game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.EraseAllPaint:FireServer()
	end
end

module.PlacePaint = function(plr, canvas, position, mode, brickcolor)
	local paintmodel = game.ReplicatedStorage.ArtCanvases:FindFirstChild(plr.Name) or workspace.ArtCanvases:FindFirstChild(plr.Name) --holds all of the player's paint parts
	if paintmodel and canvas and position and mode then
		local owner = canvas.CanvasOwner.Value
		if owner ~= nil and owner ~= plr then
			Notification(plr, "Another player has claimed this canvas. Try painting on a different canvas!")
			return
		end
		for _,v in pairs(module.canvases) do
			if v ~= canvas and v.CanvasOwner.Value == plr then
				Notification(plr, "You are already painting on another canvas. Erase the paint from your old canvas first.")
				return
			end
		end
		if owner == nil then
			canvas.CanvasOwner.Value = plr
		end
			
		local paintcount = #paintmodel:GetChildren()
		if mode == "Paint" and paintcount >= maxpaint then
			Notification(plr, "You've used up all of your available paint. Erase some parts if you wish to continue painting more!")
			return
		end
		--[[if game:GetService("RunService"):IsServer() and mode == "Paint" and paintcount > 175 then
			module.CheckForEventWin(plr)
		end]]
		
		coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(ApplyPaint),plr,paintmodel,canvas,position,mode,brickcolor)
		--ApplyPaint(plr,paintmodel,canvas,position,mode,brickcolor)

	end
end

return module

--Made by Luckymaxer

Tool = script.Parent
Handle = Tool:WaitForChild("Handle")
Mesh = Handle:WaitForChild("Mesh")

Players = game:GetService("Players")
Debris = game:GetService("Debris")

RemoteControl = Tool:WaitForChild("RemoteControl")

ServerControl = (RemoteControl:FindFirstChild("ServerControl") or Instance.new("RemoteFunction"))
ServerControl.Name = "ServerControl"
ServerControl.Parent = RemoteControl

ClientControl = (RemoteControl:FindFirstChild("ClientControl") or Instance.new("RemoteFunction"))
ClientControl.Name = "ClientControl"
ClientControl.Parent = RemoteControl

SkateboardModule = require(633112728)
SkateboardFunctions = SkateboardModule.SkateboardFunctions
Functions = SkateboardModule.Functions

Skateboard = SkateboardFunctions.GetSkateboard("Classic")
SkateboardFunctions.CreateConfiguration(Tool, Skateboard)

Sounds = {
	Drop = Handle:WaitForChild("Drop")
}

ToolEquipped = false
ToolEnabled = true

Tool.Enabled = true

ServerControl.OnServerInvoke = (function(player, Mode, Value)
end)

function InvokeClient(Mode, Value)
	local ClientReturn = nil
	pcall(function()
		ClientReturn = ClientControl:InvokeClient(Player, Mode, Value)
	end)
	return ClientReturn
end

function DropSkateboard()
	
	local MousePosition = InvokeClient("MousePosition")
	
	local DesiredAngle = (math.acos((MousePosition - Handle.Position).unit:Dot(Torso.CFrame.lookVector)))
	if DesiredAngle > (math.pi / 2) then --Prevents spawning behind character.
		--return
	end
	
	local SkateboardSize = Skateboard:GetModelSize()	
	
	local Direction = CFrame.new(Torso.Position, Vector3.new(MousePosition.X, Torso.Position.Y, MousePosition.Z))
	local SkateboardCFrame = Direction + Direction.lookVector * (((Torso.Size.Z / 2) + (SkateboardSize.Z / 2)) * 1.5)
	local SkateboardAngle = CFrame.Angles(0, 0, 0)
	
	local RayOffset = Vector3.new(-1.5, 0, -1.5)
	
	local RayPoints = { --Border points and center of skateboard used for casting rays.
		Center = Functions.PositionPart(SkateboardCFrame, 0, 0, 0),
		BackLeft = Functions.PositionPart(SkateboardCFrame, -((SkateboardSize.X / 2) + RayOffset.X), RayOffset.Y, -((SkateboardSize.Z / 2) + RayOffset.Z)),
		BackRight = Functions.PositionPart(SkateboardCFrame, ((SkateboardSize.X / 2) + RayOffset.X), RayOffset.Y, -((SkateboardSize.Z / 2) + RayOffset.Z)),
		FrontLeft = Functions.PositionPart(SkateboardCFrame, -((SkateboardSize.X / 2) + RayOffset.X), RayOffset.Y, ((SkateboardSize.Z / 2) + RayOffset.Z)),
		FrontRight = Functions.PositionPart(SkateboardCFrame, ((SkateboardSize.X / 2) + RayOffset.X), RayOffset.Y, ((SkateboardSize.Z / 2) + RayOffset.Z)),
	}
	
	local RayData = {
		Area = { --Cast ray to determine if skateboard will spawn inside something.
			Offset = Vector3.new(0, (SkateboardSize.Y / 2), 0),
			Direction = Vector3.new(0, 1, 0),
			Distance = SkateboardSize.Y
		},
		Ground = { --Cast ray to determine if the skateboard can spawn on the ground.
			Direction = Vector3.new(0, -1, 0),
			Distance = (SkateboardSize.Y * 4)
		}
	}
	
	local Ignore = {Character, Skateboard}
	
	local HighestGroundRayHit, HighestGroundRayPos --Get the highest point of elevation to spawn the skateboard.
	for i, v in pairs(RayPoints) do
		local GroundRayHit, GroundRayPos = Functions.RayCast(v.p, RayData.Ground.Direction, RayData.Ground.Distance, Ignore)
		if not HighestGroundRayPos or GroundRayPos.Y > HighestGroundRayPos.Y then
			HighestGroundRayHit, HighestGroundRayPos = GroundRayHit, GroundRayPos
		end
	end	
	
	local FullAreaIsEmpty = true --Ensure entire area is empty.
	for i, v in pairs(RayPoints) do
		local AreaIsEmpty = Functions.RegionEmpty((v.p - RayData.Area.Offset), {Min = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0), Max = (RayData.Area.Direction * RayData.Area.Distance)}, Ignore)
		if not AreaIsEmpty then
			FullAreaIsEmpty = false
		end
	end
	
	if not FullAreaIsEmpty or not HighestGroundRayHit then
		return
	end
	
	ToolEnabled = false
	Tool.Enabled = false
	
	local PosX, PosY, PosZ, R00, R01, R02, R10, R11, R12, R20, R21, R22 = SkateboardCFrame:components()
	SkateboardCFrame = CFrame.new(PosX, (HighestGroundRayPos.Y + (SkateboardSize.Y / 2)), PosZ, R00, R01, R02, R10, R11, R12, R20, R21, R22)
	
	InvokeClient("PlaySound", Sounds.Drop)
	
	Tool.Parent = nil
	
	SkateboardFunctions.SpawnSkateboard(Skateboard, (SkateboardCFrame * SkateboardAngle))
	
	if Skateboard:FindFirstChild("SkateboardPlatform") then
		Skateboard.SkateboardPlatform.CustomPhysicalProperties = PhysicalProperties.new(4,.7,.2)
	end

	Debris:AddItem(Tool, 0)	
	Tool:Destroy()
	
end

function CheckIfAlive()
	return (((Player and Player.Parent and Character and Character.Parent and Humanoid and Humanoid.Parent and Humanoid.Health > 0 and Torso and Torso.Parent) and true) or false)
end

function Activated()
	if not ToolEquipped or not ToolEnabled or not CheckIfAlive() then
		return
	end
	DropSkateboard()
end

function Equipped()
	Character = Tool.Parent
	Player = Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(Character)
	Humanoid = Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
	Torso = Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if not CheckIfAlive() then
		return
	end
	ToolEquipped = true
end

function Unequipped()
	ToolEquipped = false
end

Tool.Activated:connect(Activated)
Tool.Equipped:connect(Equipped)
Tool.Unequipped:connect(Unequipped)
=== chunk=595 type=PROP offset=57521 runSha256=62f28a45937cd004d9460c29bed8cb2730ed82eb51573a6a6e9aca5186c18ac3 ===
function weld()
	local parts,last = {}
	local function scan(parent)
		for _,v in pairs(parent:GetChildren()) do
			if (v:IsA("BasePart")) then
				if (last) then
					local w = Instance.new("Weld")
					w.Name = ("%s_Weld"):format(v.Name)
					w.Part0,w.Part1 = last,v
					w.C0 = last.CFrame:inverse()
					w.C1 = v.CFrame:inverse()
					w.Parent = last
				end
				last = v
				table.insert(parts,v)
			end
			scan(v)
		end
	end
	scan(script.Parent.Parent)
	for _,v in pairs(parts) do
		v.Anchored = false
	end
end

weld()
script:Destroy()

			return v

	end

			game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.RemoveSpecialStatus:Fire(plr,"CarUnlocked")
		end

local cooldown = {}

function CheckCooldown(plr)
	for _,v in pairs(cooldown) do
		if v == plr.Name then
			return false
		end
	end
	return true
end

function AddToCooldown(plr)
	table.insert(cooldown,plr.Name)
	wait(.5)
	table.remove(cooldown,1)
end

game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.SpawnCarRequest.OnServerInvoke = function(plr,position)
	local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)
	if data and CheckCooldown(plr) and plr.Character and plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and (plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - position.p).magnitude < 52 then
		coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(AddToCooldown),plr)
		for i,v in pairs(game.Workspace:GetChildren()) do
			if v.Name == "vehicle_"..plr.Name then
				v:Destroy()
			end
		end
		
		game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.RemoveSpecialStatus:Fire(plr,"CarLocked")
		game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.RemoveSpecialStatus:Fire(plr,"DriverUnlocked")
		game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.AddSpecialStatus:Fire(plr,"CarUnlocked")
		game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.AddSpecialStatus:Fire(plr,"DriverLocked")
		
		local findcar = getItemDataFromID(plr.SelectedCar.Value)
		local paint = getItemDataFromID(plr.SelectedPaint.Value)
		
		if data.Objects:FindFirstChild(plr.SelectedCar.Value) == nil or (paint ~= nil and paint.Name:match("paint_") and data.Objects:FindFirstChild(plr.SelectedPaint.Value) == nil) then print("car spawn fail "..plr.Name) return end --bad!
		
		if findcar then
			
			local boxsize,boxcframe = require(game.ReplicatedStorage.Modules.GetBoundingBox)(findcar)
			local bb = Instance.new("Part")
			bb.Transparency = 1
			bb.Anchored = true
			bb.Size = boxsize
			bb.Parent = workspace
			bb.CFrame = CFrame.new(position.p + Vector3.new(0,boxsize.y / 2,0))
			local parts = bb:GetTouchingParts()
			bb:Destroy()
			for _,v in pairs(parts) do
				if v.Parent:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") or (v.Parent:FindFirstChild("DriverLocked") and v.Parent:FindFirstChild("Owner") and v.Parent.Owner.Value ~= plr.Name) then --too close to something else
					game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireClient(plr,"You cannot spawn your car here, as it is too close to yourself or another car/person.")
					return
				end
			end
			
			local car = findcar:clone()
			car.Name = "vehicle_"..plr.Name
			
			local owner = car:FindFirstChild("Owner")
			if not owner then
				owner = Instance.new("StringValue")
				owner.Name = "Owner"
			end
			owner.Value = plr.Name
			owner.Parent = car
			
			local ca = Instance.new("StringValue")
			ca.Name = "ContextActionsModelName"
			ca.Value = "Vehicle"
			ca.Parent = car
			
			if paint then
				for i,v in pairs(car:GetChildren()) do
					if v.Name == "Paintable" then
						v.BrickColor = paint.BrickColorValue.Value
						v.Reflectance = paint.Reflectance.Value
						v.Transparency = paint.Transparency.Value
					end
					local x = v:FindFirstChild("Sparkles")
					if x then
						x.SparkleColor = paint.BrickColorValue.Value.Color
					end
					local y = v:FindFirstChild("SpecialLight")
					if y then
						y.Color = paint.BrickColorValue.Value.Color
					end
				end
			end
			car.DetectLeave.Disabled = false
			
			local engine = car:FindFirstChild("Engine") or car:FindFirstChild("Anchor")
			car.PrimaryPart = engine
			for i,v in pairs(car:GetChildren()) do
				if v:IsA("Part") and v.Shape == Enum.PartType.Cylinder then
					local weld = Instance.new("Weld") 
					weld.Part0 = engine
					weld.Part1 = v 
					weld.C0 = engine.CFrame:inverse() 
					weld.C1 = v.CFrame:inverse() 
					weld.Parent = v
				end
			end
			car.Parent = game.Workspace
			car:MakeJoints()
			car:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(CFrame.new(position.p + Vector3.new(0,6,0)))
			--car.Engine.CFrame = CFrame.new(position.p + Vector3.new(0,8,0))
			wait(.25)
			for i,v in pairs(car:GetChildren()) do
				if v:IsA("Part") and v.Shape == Enum.PartType.Cylinder and v:FindFirstChild("Weld") then
					v.Weld:Destroy()
				end
			end
			return true
		end
	end
end

	zones[zone.Name] = {}
	for _,part in pairs(zone:GetChildren()) do
		local lx,ly,lz = math.huge,math.huge,math.huge
		local mx,my,mz = -math.huge,-math.huge,-math.huge
		local function ApplyValues(p)
			if p.x < lx then lx = p.x end
			if p.x > mx then mx = p.x end
			if p.y < ly then ly = p.y end
			if p.y > my then my = p.y end
			if p.z < lz then lz = p.z end
			if p.z > mz then mz = p.z end
		end
		local points = {}

		local cf1 = part.CFrame
		local cf2 = (part.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(0,math.pi/2,0))
		local cf3 = (part.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(math.pi/2,0,0))
		local lvz = (cf1.lookVector * (part.Size.Z/2))
		local lvx = (cf2.lookVector * (part.Size.X/2))
		local lvy = (cf3.lookVector * (part.Size.Y/2))
		points[1] = cf1+lvz
		points[2] = cf1-lvz
		points[3] = cf2+lvx
		points[4] = cf2-lvx
		points[5] = cf3+lvy
		points[6] = cf3-lvy
		for _,p in pairs(points) do
			ApplyValues(p)
		end 
		table.insert(zones[zone.Name],{["Coordinates"] = {lx=lx,ly=ly,lz=lz, mx=mx,my=my,mz=mz}})
	end
end

function IsInZone(p, zonedata)
	for _,data in pairs(zonedata) do
		if data["Coordinates"] then
			local t = data["Coordinates"]
			if (p.x > t.lx and p.x < t.mx and p.y > t.ly and p.y < t.my and p.z > t.lz and p.z < t.mz) then --is the character within all the coordinates of the zone?
				return true
			end
		end
	end
	return false
end


game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.PlayerIsInZone.OnInvoke = function(plr, zonename)
	local char = plr.Character
	if char then
		local torso = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if torso then
			local p = torso.Position
			local zonedata = zones[zonename]
			if zonedata then
				return IsInZone(p, zonedata)
			end
		end
	end
end


--[[game.Lighting.Changed:connect(function(property)
	if property == "TimeOfDay" then
		local period = game.ReplicatedStorage.Period.Value
		if period >= 1 and period <= 6 and period ~= 4 then
			local classname = game.ReplicatedStorage.Schedule["P"..period].Value
			if classname then
				local zone = zones[classname]
				if zone then
					for _,plr in pairs(game.Players:GetChildren()) do
						local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)
						if data then
							local hasbadge = data.HasBadge.Value

										end
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
end)]]

local gps = game:GetService("GamePassService")
local housepassid = game.ReplicatedStorage.GamePasses.House.Value
local storage = game.ReplicatedStorage.Objects

local bannedcolors = {"Really red","New Yeller","Really blue","Lime green","Hot pink","Toothpaste","Teal"}
local exteriorparts = {"Foundation","Deck","OuterParts","ExteriorWall","Roof"}

local objectsready = false

function createWeld(mainpart,target)
	local weld = Instance.new("Weld") 
	weld.Part0 = mainpart
	weld.Part1 = target 
	weld.C0 = mainpart.CFrame:inverse() 
	weld.C1 = target.CFrame:inverse() 
	weld.Parent = target
	target.Anchored = false
end

function getItemIDByName(itemname)
	for _,v in pairs(game.ReplicatedStorage.Objects:GetChildren()) do
		if v.Name == itemname then
			return v.ItemID.Value
		end
	end
end


function getFurnitureCount(plr)
	local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)
	local house = workspace:FindFirstChild("!home_"..plr.Name)
	if data and house then
		local val = data:FindFirstChild("FurnitureCount")
		if val == nil then
			val = Instance.new("IntValue",data)
			val.Name = "FurnitureCount"
		end
		local fcount = 0
		for i,v in pairs(house:GetChildren()) do
			if v:IsA("Model") then
				fcount = fcount + 1
			end
		end
		val.Value = fcount
		return fcount
	end
end


game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.ToggleHomeLock.OnServerEvent:connect(function(plr,home) --can be home or apartment
	if home.Name:match(plr.Name) then
		local door = home.Door
		local lock = home:FindFirstChild("LockButton",true)
		if door.CanCollide == false then
			door.CanCollide = true
			lock.BrickColor = BrickColor.new("Bright red")
		else
			door.CanCollide = false
			lock.BrickColor = BrickColor.new("Bright green")
		end
	end
end)


game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.ToggleRestrictLock.OnServerEvent:connect(function(plr,home)
	if home.Name:match(plr.Name) then
		local restrict = home.Restrictions
		if restrict.Value == true then
			restrict.Value = false
		else
			restrict.Value = true
		end
	end
end)


game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.ToggleHomeRestrictedPlayer.OnServerInvoke = function(plr,home,targetname) --okay, really we're adding people to the whitelist, not restricting them
	if home.Name:match(plr.Name) then
		local restrict = home.Restrictions
		local targetplr
		for _,v in pairs(game.Players:GetChildren()) do
			if string.lower(targetname) == string.lower(v.Name) then
				targetplr = v
			end
		end
		if targetplr then
			if targetplr ~= plr then
				if restrict:FindFirstChild(targetplr.Name) then
					restrict[targetplr.Name]:Destroy()
					return true
				else
					local marker = Instance.new("Folder",restrict)
					marker.Name = targetplr.Name
					return true
				end
			else
				return false,"Can't whitelist yourself"
			end
		else
			return false,"Player not in server"
		end
	end
end


function getFurnitureCostMarker(stats,servobj)
	local furnbought
	local id = servobj.ItemID.Value
	if stats.FurnitureCost:FindFirstChild(id) then
		furnbought = stats.FurnitureCost:FindFirstChild(id)
	else
		furnbought = Instance.new("IntValue")
		furnbought.Value = 0
		furnbought.Name = id
		furnbought.Parent = stats.FurnitureCost
	end
	local pending
	if furnbought:FindFirstChild("PendingSales") then
		pending = furnbought.PendingSales
	else
		pending = Instance.new("IntValue")
		pending.Value = 0
		pending.Name = "PendingSales"
		pending.Parent = furnbought
	end
	return furnbought,pending
end


function getOwnedObjectCount(plr,itemid)
	local count = 0
	local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)
	if data then
		for _,v in pairs(data.Objects:GetChildren()) do
			if tonumber(v.Name) == itemid then
				count = count + 1
			end
		end
	end
	return count
end


coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(function() --initialize all furniture models
	for _,v in pairs(game.ReplicatedStorage.Objects:GetChildren()) do
		if v.Name:match("furni_") then
			coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(function()
			local model
			repeat
				local success = pcall(function()
					model = game:GetService("InsertService"):LoadAsset(v.ModelID.Value):GetChildren()[1]
				end)
				if not success then print("Failed to load "..v.Name) wait(1) end
			until model ~= nil
			
			local pp = model.PrimaryPart
			for _,part in pairs(model:GetChildren()) do
				local weld = part:FindFirstChild("Weld")
				if weld then
					part.Anchored = true
					weld:Destroy()
				end
				if part:IsA("BasePart") then
					part.Parent = v
				end
			end
			
			v.PrimaryPart = pp
			for _,part in pairs(v:GetChildren()) do
				if part:IsA("Seat") and part.Anchored == false then
					createWeld(pp,part)
				end
			end
			print("Loaded furniture "..model.Name)
			model:Destroy()
			end))
		end
	end
	local complete = true
	repeat
		wait(5)
		complete = true
		for _,v in pairs(game.ReplicatedStorage.Objects:GetChildren()) do
			if v.Name:match("furni_") and v.PrimaryPart == nil then
				complete = false
			end
		end
	until complete == true
	objectsready = true
	print("All furniture loaded and ready")
end))

function loadHouse(plr,placement)
	if not script:FindFirstChild("load_"..plr.Name) then
		if objectsready == false then
			repeat wait(math.random()) until objectsready == true
		end
		local pid = plr.userId
		local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:WaitForChild(pid)
		local passes = data:WaitForChild("GamePasses")
		local hp = passes:WaitForChild("House")
		if plr and (game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.GamePassCheck:Invoke(plr,"House") == true or game:GetService("GamePassService"):PlayerHasPass(plr,housepassid) == true or data.Objects:FindFirstChild("183")) then
			local pd = game:GetService("DataStoreService"):GetDataStore(pid.."_data")
			
			--the table name is the	unique name, like Object_1, to load each indiv obj
			--the Name key is the name of the object in ServerStorage (to load it)		
			
			
			local housedata 
			local success = pcall(function()
				housedata = pd:GetAsync("house")
			end)
			if housedata == nil then
				housedata = {}
			end
			
			if not success then
				wait(2)
				game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireClient(plr,"Your house failed to load for this session. ROBLOX's servers may be experiencing issues; please try again later.",true)
				return
			end

			if data:FindFirstChild("FurnitureCost") then
				data.FurnitureCost.Value = 0
				data.FurnitureCost:ClearAllChildren()
			end
			
			local house = game.ServerStorage.BaseHouse:clone()
			house.PrimaryPart = house.Floor
			
			for i,obj in pairs(housedata) do --load all furniture and house data
				
				if housedata[i]["Name"] ~= nil and storage:FindFirstChild(housedata[i]["Name"]) ~= nil then
					local model = storage:FindFirstChild(housedata[i]["Name"]):clone()
					model.Name = housedata[i]["Name"]
					model.Parent = house
					if model:IsA("Model") then
						model:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(house.Floor.CFrame + (Vector3.new(housedata[i]["CFrame"][1],housedata[i]["CFrame"][2],housedata[i]["CFrame"][3])))
						model:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(model.PrimaryPart.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(0,math.rad(housedata[i]["CFrame"][4]),0))
						model.RotValue.Value = housedata[i]["CFrame"][4]
						model.PaintColor.Value = BrickColor.new(housedata[i]["PaintColor"])
						for i,part in pairs(model:GetChildren()) do
							if part:IsA("BasePart") and (part.Name == "Paintable") then
								part.BrickColor = model.PaintColor.Value
							end
						end
					end
				end
	
			end --end the furniture loading
			
			if housedata["MainHouseData"] ~= nil then
			
				for i,v in pairs(housedata["MainHouseData"]) do
					for _,part in pairs(house:GetChildren()) do
						if part:IsA("BasePart") and part.Name == i then
							part.BrickColor = BrickColor.new(v)
						end
					end
				end
			
			end
			
			local nametag = house:FindFirstChild("NameTag",true)
			nametag.TextLabel.Text = plr.Name
			
			if workspace:FindFirstChild("!home_"..plr.Name) then
				workspace.HousePlacement[workspace["!home_"..plr.Name].HousePlacement.Value].Occupied.Value = false
				workspace["!home_"..plr.Name]:Destroy()
			end
			
			house["Lock Button"].Owner.Value = plr.Name
			house["Restrict Button"].Owner.Value = plr.Name
			
			if placement == nil then
				for i=1,20 do
					if workspace.HousePlacement[i].Occupied.Value == false then
						workspace.HousePlacement[i].Occupied.Value = true
						house.Parent = workspace
						house.Name = "!home_"..plr.Name
						house:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(workspace.HousePlacement[i].CFrame)
						local placevalue = Instance.new("IntValue")
						placevalue.Name = "HousePlacement"
						placevalue.Value = i
						placevalue.Parent = house
						break
					end
				end
			else
				workspace.HousePlacement[placement].Occupied.Value = true
				house.Parent = workspace
				house.Name = "!home_"..plr.Name
				house:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(workspace.HousePlacement[placement].CFrame)
				local placevalue = Instance.new("IntValue")
				placevalue.Name = "HousePlacement"
				placevalue.Value = placement
				placevalue.Parent = house
				if plr and plr:FindFirstChild("Backpack") and plr:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui") and plr.Character and plr.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
					for i,v in pairs(plr.Backpack:GetChildren()) do
						if v.Name == "Move" or v.Name == "Paint" or v.Name == "Remove" then
							v:Destroy()
						end
					end
					for i,v in pairs(plr.Character:GetChildren()) do
						if (v.Name == "Move" or v.Name == "Paint" or v.Name == "Remove") and v:IsA("Tool") then
							plr.Character.Humanoid:UnequipTools()
							wait()
							v:Destroy()
						end
					end
					--[[for i,v in pairs(plr.PlayerGui:GetChildren()) do
						if v:IsA("SelectionBox") or v:IsA("Handles") or v.Name == "RemoveGui" or v.Name == "MoveToolGui" then
							v:Destroy()
						end
					end]] --can't do that
				end
			end
			
			--[[for i,v in pairs(house:GetChildren()) do
				if v:IsA("Model") then
					for i,part in pairs(v:GetChildren()) do
						if part:IsA("BasePart") and not part:IsA("Seat") then
							part.Anchored = true
						end
					end
				end
			end
			for i,v in pairs(house.Floor:GetChildren()) do
				if v:IsA("Weld") then
					v:Destroy()
				end
			end]]
			
			local loadmarker = Instance.new("BoolValue")
			loadmarker.Name = "load_"..plr.Name
			loadmarker.Parent = script
			game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(loadmarker,12)
			
			if plr.Parent == nil then
				workspace.HousePlacement[house.HousePlacement.Value].Occupied.Value = false
				house:Destroy()
				return false
			end
			
			getFurnitureCount(plr)
			
			return true
			
		end --end the entire house loading function
	else
		return false
	end
end

game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.ReloadHouse.OnServerInvoke = function(plr,placement)
	local request = loadHouse(plr,placement)
	return request
end

game.Players.ChildAdded:connect(function(plr)

function RemoveFurnitureItem(plr,model) --referenced later down
	local house = workspace:FindFirstChild("!home_"..plr.Name)
	local stats = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)
	if house and stats and model.Parent == house then
		local itemserv = game.ReplicatedStorage.Objects:FindFirstChild(model.Name)
		if itemserv then
			local furnbought,pending = getFurnitureCostMarker(stats,itemserv)
			furnbought.Value = furnbought.Value + 1
			model:Destroy()
			if getOwnedObjectCount(plr,itemserv.ItemID.Value) + furnbought.Value <= 0 then
				stats.FurnitureCost.Value = stats.FurnitureCost.Value - itemserv.ItemCost.Value
			end
			getFurnitureCount(plr)
			return true
		end
	end
end

				local stats = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)

										if val == tonumber(v.Name) then
											table.remove(tbl["objects"],i)
											--need to make sure we get the unique objects out of the player's inv...
											--cant call findfirstchild ONLY, that makes it so only 1 gets removed
											for _,x in pairs(stats.Objects:GetChildren()) do
												if x.Name == v.Name then
													local matches
													for _,z in pairs(markerstodestroy) do
														if z == x then
															matches = true
														end
													end
													if not matches then
														--it is unique, not already being destroyed
														table.insert(markerstodestroy, x)
														break
													end
												end
											end
											break
										end
									end
								end
							end
							
						end
						
						tbl["cash"] = tbl["cash"] - stats.FurnitureCost.Value
						
						return tbl
					end)
					
					if stats and savesuccess then
						for _,v in pairs(markerstocreate) do
							local marker = Instance.new("StringValue",stats.Objects)
							marker.Name = v
						end
						for _,v in pairs(markerstodestroy) do
							v:Destroy()
						end
						
						stats.cash.Value = stats.cash.Value - stats.FurnitureCost.Value
						stats.oldcash.Value = stats.oldcash.Value - stats.FurnitureCost.Value
						
						stats.FurnitureCost.Value = 0
						for _,v in pairs(stats.FurnitureCost:GetChildren()) do
							v:Destroy()
						end
					end
					
					local savemarker = Instance.new("BoolValue")
					savemarker.Name = "save_"..plr.Name
					savemarker.Parent = script
					game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(savemarker,18)
					return true
				end
			end) --end the save function
			
			house:Destroy() --remove the cloned house
			
			--[[if plr and plr:FindFirstChild("Backpack") then
				for i,v in pairs(tools) do
					v.Enabled = true
				end
			end]]
			--house.Parent = workspace
			
			if savefunc == true then
				return true
			end
		end
	else
		return false --please wait
	end
end



game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.GetFurniture.OnServerInvoke = function(plr,objname)
	local house = workspace:FindFirstChild("!home_"..plr.Name)
	local stats = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)
	if plr.Character and plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and house and house.Floor.Editing.Value == true and stats then
		local torso = plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart
		local ray = Ray.new((torso.CFrame + torso.CFrame.lookVector * 10).p, (((torso.CFrame + torso.CFrame.lookVector * 10).p + Vector3.new(0,-15,0)) - (torso.CFrame + torso.CFrame.lookVector * 10).p).unit*15)
		local ignoredparts = {}
		for i,v in pairs(house:GetChildren()) do
			if v:IsA("Model") then
				table.insert(ignoredparts,v)
			end
		end
		--check if ray is inside house
		local findpart = workspace:FindPartOnRayWithIgnoreList(ray,ignoredparts)
		if findpart == nil or findpart.Parent.Name ~= house.Name or not findpart.Name:match("Floor") then
			return false,"Please stand facing some empty space inside your house, then try again."
		end
		local fcount = getFurnitureCount(plr)
		if fcount == 450 then
			game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireClient(plr, "Notice: You are approaching the item limit for your house. You have placed "..fcount.."/500 items.")
		end
		if fcount >= 500 then
			--hoarder!!
			return false,"Sorry, you cannot fit more than 1000 items in your home!"
		end
		
		
		local components = (torso.CFrame + torso.CFrame.lookVector * 10).p
		local itemserv = game.ReplicatedStorage.Objects:FindFirstChild(objname)
		if itemserv then
			local item = itemserv:clone()
			for _,v in pairs(item:GetChildren()) do
				if v.ClassName:match("Value") and v.Name ~= "PaintColor" and v.Name ~= "RotValue" and v.Name ~= "ItemID" then
					v:Destroy()
				end
			end
			item.Parent = house
			
			item:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(house.Floor.CFrame)
			local boxsize,boxcframe = require(game.ReplicatedStorage.Modules.GetBoundingBox)(item)
			local bottomboundary = boxcframe.Y - boxsize.Y / 2
			local yoffset = item.PrimaryPart.Position.Y - bottomboundary + house.Floor.Size.Y / 2
			local origpos = item.PrimaryPart.Position
			item:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(item.PrimaryPart.CFrame + (Vector3.new(math.floor(components.X),yoffset,math.floor(components.Z))) - Vector3.new(origpos.X,0,origpos.Z)) -- orig CFrame + (newpos - oldpos)

			local tempval = Instance.new("BoolValue")
			tempval.Name = "temp"
			tempval.Parent = item
			local furnbought,pending = getFurnitureCostMarker(stats,itemserv)
			furnbought.Value = furnbought.Value - 1
			if getOwnedObjectCount(plr,itemserv.ItemID.Value) + pending.Value + furnbought.Value < 0 then
				stats.FurnitureCost.Value = stats.FurnitureCost.Value + itemserv.ItemCost.Value
			end
			getFurnitureCount(plr)
			return true
		end
		
	end
end

game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.RemoveFurniture.OnServerEvent:connect(function(plr,model)
	RemoveFurnitureItem(plr,model)
end)

game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.SellFurniture.OnServerInvoke = function(plr,objname)
	local stats = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)
	if stats then
		local servobj = game.ReplicatedStorage.Objects:FindFirstChild(objname)
		--local obj = stats.Objects:FindFirstChild(objname)
		if servobj and not servobj:FindFirstChild("TwitterCode") and not servobj:FindFirstChild("NotForSale") then
			local furnbought,pending = getFurnitureCostMarker(stats,servobj)
			if getOwnedObjectCount(plr,servobj.ItemID.Value) + pending.Value + furnbought.Value > 0 then
				pending.Value = pending.Value - 1
				stats.FurnitureCost.Value = stats.FurnitureCost.Value - math.ceil(servobj.ItemCost.Value * .7)
				return true
			end
		end
	end
end





game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.PaintHouseItem.OnServerEvent:connect(function(plr,item,color)
	if item.Parent.Name == "!home_"..plr.Name then
		if item:IsA("Model") then
			item.PaintColor.Value = color
			for i,v in pairs(item:GetChildren()) do
				if v:IsA("BasePart") and v.Name == "Paintable" then
					v.BrickColor = color
				end
			end
		elseif item:IsA("BasePart") then
			local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)
			
			local function paintParts(part,cl)
				for i,v in pairs(part.Parent:GetChildren()) do
					if v:IsA("BasePart") and v.Name == part.Name then
						v.BrickColor = cl
					end
				end
			end
			
			local colorbanned = false
			for _,v in pairs(bannedcolors) do
				if color.Name == v then
					colorbanned = true
				end
			end
			
			local isexterior = false
			for _,v in pairs(exteriorparts) do
				if item.Name == v then
					isexterior = true
				end
			end
			
			if not isexterior then
				paintParts(item,color)
			else
				if (data and (data.Objects:FindFirstChild("208") or game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.GamePassCheck:Invoke(plr,"House"))) and colorbanned == false then
					paintParts(item,color)
				elseif colorbanned then
					paintParts(item,item.BrickColor)
					game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireClient(plr, "You're not allowed to paint your exterior walls with neon colors!")
				else
					paintParts(item,item.BrickColor)
					game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.OpenGuiFromServer:FireClient(plr,"BuyPaintingLicense")
				end
			end
			
			
		end
	end
end)



game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.SendNewFurniturePosition.OnServerEvent:connect(function(plr,selectedmodel,newcframe,newrotval)
	if selectedmodel then
		local pp = selectedmodel.PrimaryPart
		local ray = Ray.new(newcframe.p, ((newcframe.p + Vector3.new(0,-15,0)) - newcframe.p).unit*10)
		local ignoredparts = {}
		for i,v in pairs(selectedmodel.Parent:GetChildren()) do
			if v:IsA("Model") then
				table.insert(ignoredparts,v)
			end
		end
		local findpart = workspace:FindPartOnRayWithIgnoreList(ray,ignoredparts)
		if findpart == nil or not findpart.Name:match("Floor") then --put it back in orig pos for the client
			selectedmodel:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(pp.CFrame)
			selectedmodel.RotValue.Value = selectedmodel.RotValue.Value
			game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireClient(plr,"An error occurred while trying to position this piece of furniture. Please try again.")
		else --apply the new position and rotation
			selectedmodel:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(newcframe)
			selectedmodel.RotValue.Value = newrotval
		end
	end
end)



game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.EnterHouseEditMode.OnServerEvent:connect(function(plr)
	local house = workspace:FindFirstChild("!home_"..plr.Name)
	if house then
		house.Floor.Editing.Value = true
	end
end)


--"exit house" is just ReloadHouse(), nothing else needed


game.Players.ChildRemoved:connect(function(plr)
	local house = workspace:FindFirstChild("!home_"..plr.Name)
	if house then
		workspace.HousePlacement[house.HousePlacement.Value].Occupied.Value = false
		house:Destroy()
	end
end)
